/* Goal-aware draft planning. Writes use the existing permission-enforcing APIs. */
(() => {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__cvGoalPlanner) return;
  const ROOT = '/communiverse/';
  const text = (v, limit = 2500) => typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim().slice(0, limit) : '';
  const uuid = v => /^[a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12}$/i.test(v || '');
  const el = (tag, cls, value) => { const n = document.createElement(tag); if (cls) n.className = cls; if (value !== undefined) n.textContent = value; return n; };
  const button = (label, action, primary = false) => { const b = el('button', 'cvgp-action' + (primary ? ' is-primary' : ''), label); b.type = 'button'; b.addEventListener('click', action); return b; };
  const glyph = kind => { const s = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'svg'); for (const [k,v] of Object.entries({viewBox:'0 0 24 24',fill:'none',stroke:'currentColor','stroke-width':'1.7','stroke-linecap':'round','stroke-linejoin':'round','aria-hidden':'true'})) s.setAttribute(k,v); const p = document.createElementNS(s.namespaceURI,'path'); p.setAttribute('d',kind==='trash'?'M3 6h18M9 6V4h6v2M5 6l1 14h12l1-14M10 10v6M14 10v6':kind==='close'?'M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18':'M6 9l6 6 6-6'); s.append(p); return s; };
  const recruitment = prompt => /\bamb[a-z]*[ds][a-z]*r?s?\b/i.test(prompt) && (/\b(recruit\w*|hir\w*)\b/i.test(prompt) || /\b(?:look\w*|find\w*|seek\w*|search\w*|need\w*|want\w*|get)(?:\s+(?:for|more|new|some|additional|\d+|an?)){0,5}\s+amb[a-z]*[ds][a-z]*r?s?\b/i.test(prompt));
  const meetingTopic = prompt => /\b(meet(?:ings?)?|catch[ -]?up|working lunch|team lunch|dinner meeting)\b/i.test(prompt);
  const meetingGoal = prompt => meetingTopic(prompt)
    && !/^\s*(?:show|list|find|search|when is|when are|where is|where are|who is|who are|cancel|delete|reschedule)\b/i.test(prompt)
    && !/\b(?:(?:want|need|would like)\s+to\s+(?:know|see|check|find out)|(?:do not|don't|no longer)\s+(?:want|need|plan))\b/i.test(prompt)
    && /\b(want\w*|wnat|wanna|need\w*|plan\w*|organis\w*|organiz\w*|arrang\w*|schedul\w*|set up|hold|host|book|prepar\w*|create\w*|let[’']?s|can (?:i|we|you)|could (?:i|we|you))\b/i.test(prompt);
  const PROCESS_RELEASE='20261012-process-cards-v3';
  const personnelGoal = prompt => !/\b(?:fire (?:up|a kiln|the kiln|kilns?|ceramics?|pottery|clay|alarm|safety)|firing (?:clay|pottery|ceramics?|a kiln|the kiln)|(?:don't|do not|never) (?:fire|dismiss|terminate))\b/i.test(prompt) && /\b(?:fire\s+\p{L}+|dismiss\w*|offboard\w*|let\s+\p{L}+[\p{L}\s]{0,40}\s+go|end (?:the |our |a )?(?:working relationship|contract|engagement)|terminat\w* \p{L}+)\b/iu.test(prompt);
  const shouldPlan = prompt => personnelGoal(prompt) || (meetingTopic(prompt) ? meetingGoal(prompt) : recruitment(prompt) || /\b(plan\w*|organis\w*|organiz\w*|launch\w*|create\w*|prepar\w*|recruit\w*|look\w*|need\w*|want\w*|build\w*|develop\w*|improve\w*|train\w*|onboard\w*)\b/i.test(prompt) || /how (?:do|can|should) (?:i|we)/i.test(prompt));
  const roles={ceo:'CEO',operations:'Operations admin',ambassador:'Ambassador','ambassador-lead':'Ambassador programme lead',ai:'AI agentic layer'};
  function processContext(prompt,overview){
    const words=prompt.toLocaleLowerCase().match(/\p{L}+/gu)||[],team=overview?.team||[];
    const people=team.filter(p=>{const names=String(p.name||'').toLocaleLowerCase().match(/\p{L}+/gu)||[];return names.length&&names.every(n=>words.includes(n))});
    const target=prompt.match(/\b(?:fire|dismiss|offboard|terminate|let)\s+(\p{L}+)/iu)?.[1]?.toLocaleLowerCase(),subject=target?people.find(p=>String(p.name).toLocaleLowerCase().split(/\s+/)[0]===target):null;
    return {kind:personnelGoal(prompt)?'personnel-review':meetingGoal(prompt)?'meeting-planning':recruitment(prompt)?'recruitment':'goal',subject:subject?{id:subject.id,name:subject.name}:null,people:people.slice(0,4).map(p=>({id:p.id,name:p.name,role:roles[p.role]||p.title||p.role||'Recorded team member'})),coordinator:team.find(p=>p.role==='operations'),decisionContact:team.find(p=>p.role==='ceo')};
  }
  function safeRecord(record){
    if(!record||!text(record.title,180))return null;
    let url='';try{const u=new URL(record.url,location.origin);if(u.origin===location.origin&&u.pathname.startsWith(ROOT)&&!u.pathname.startsWith(ROOT+'api/'))url=u.pathname+u.search;}catch{}
    if(!url)return null;return {id:text(record.id,100),title:text(record.title,180),kind:text(record.kind,40),description:text(record.description,300),status:text(record.status,50),url};
  }
  function reviewPattern(pattern){
    // Generated advice cannot establish a person's approval authority.
    return {...pattern,steps:pattern.steps.map(step=>({...step,approval:true,gate:'Review this proposed output with the confirmed authorised reviewer before proceeding. The applicable terms and review route remain unconfirmed.',approvalLabel:'Confirmed human review required; no authority inferred.'}))};
  }
  // Each phase has a concrete output and editable actions; no invented dates or fees.
  const phase = (title, summary, actions, output, approval = false) => ({id:crypto.randomUUID(),title,summary,actions:actions.map(label=>({label,done:false})),output,approval,owner:'',due:''});
  function guidePlan(prompt, ids) {
    const q=prompt.toLowerCase(),guides=window.__cvOperatingGuides||[];
    const matches=guides.filter(g=>!ids||ids.includes(g.id)).map(g=>({g,score:g.terms.reduce((n,t)=>n+(new RegExp('\\b'+t.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+'\\b','i').test(q)?t.includes(' ')?5:2:0),0)})).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
    if(!matches.length)return null;const g=matches[0].g;
    return {title:g.title,guideId:g.id,intro:g.summary,steps:g.steps.map(([title,summary,output])=>phase(title,summary,[summary,'Confirm the responsible person and any missing information','Record the outcome and next step'],output,/review commitments|request approval|review.*approval|confirm decisions/i.test(title))).map(s=>({...s,approvalLabel:'Required approval before commitments.'}))};
  }
  function blueprint(prompt) {
    if (meetingGoal(prompt)) {
      const ambassadors=/\bamb[a-z]*[ds][a-z]*r?s?\b/i.test(prompt),restaurant=/\b(restaur\w*|dinner|lunch|cafe|café)\b/i.test(prompt);
      return {title:ambassadors?'Plan your ambassador meeting':'Plan your meeting',guideId:'meeting-planning',intro:restaurant?'A practical plan for meeting together at a restaurant. Confirm the people, place and time before sending invitations.':'Agree the purpose, people and arrangements, then prepare an invitation for review.',steps:[
        phase('Agree the purpose and people','Decide what the meeting should achieve and who needs to join.',[ambassadors?'Choose the ambassadors who should attend; confirm the programme lead':'Choose the participants and meeting owner','Define the decisions, updates or support needed','Ask about availability, dietary requirements and access needs'],'Meeting purpose and attendee shortlist'),
        phase(restaurant?'Find a suitable restaurant':'Choose the place and format',restaurant?'Choose a place where the group can comfortably hear and speak to one another.':'Choose an in-person venue or a suitable meeting link.',restaurant?['Confirm the city and compare quiet, accessible restaurants with enough seating','Check menu choices, dietary needs, parking or transport, and group availability','Ask for a written quote and check minimum spend, deposits and cancellation terms']:['Confirm the city or online format','Check capacity, accessibility and practical needs','Confirm venue or meeting-link availability'],'Venue shortlist and practical requirements'),
        phase('Confirm time and costs','Agree availability and review any commitments before booking.',['Collect participant availability and confirm the date, start/end time and time zone',restaurant?'Confirm headcount and the quoted meal or venue cost; leave unknown amounts unset':'Confirm headcount and any venue or equipment costs','Get the required spending approval before paying a deposit or making commitments'],'Confirmed arrangements and any required approval',true),
        phase('Prepare the agenda','Keep the discussion focused and leave space for decisions.',[ambassadors?'Cover programme priorities and a brief update from each ambassador':'Cover the meeting purpose and participant updates','Discuss blockers, ideas and support needed','Leave time to agree actions, owners and the next check-in'],'Agenda and supporting materials'),
        phase('Review and send invitations','Make it easy for people to understand and confirm the arrangements.',['Prepare the attendee list, date/time, time zone, location or map link and agenda','Review the invitation before scheduling and sending it','Track replies and reconfirm attendance and venue requirements'],'Reviewed invitations and attendance responses'),
        phase('Meet and follow up','Turn the conversation into clear next steps.',['Use the agenda and record decisions during the meeting','Confirm an owner and due date for each agreed action','Share the reviewed summary and next check-in; record any actual costs'],'Meeting notes, assigned actions and follow-up')
      ].map(s=>({...s,approvalLabel:s.approval?'Required approval before booking or spending.':''}))};
    }
    if (recruitment(prompt)) return {title:'Recruit ambassadors',intro:'A recruitment plan, from finding people to helping them succeed.',steps:[
      phase('Define the brief','Agree on the role, region and what success looks like.',['Describe who the ambassador will represent','Define responsibilities and selection criteria','Agree how the programme will measure success'],'Recruitment brief'),
      phase('Find candidates','Use community referrals, relevant networks and targeted outreach.',['Map the communities and maker networks in the target region','Prepare an outreach message and application questions','Ask for referrals and record interested candidates'],'Candidate pool'),
      phase('Review and shortlist','Assess community ties, storytelling and availability.',['Use the same role-based scorecard for each candidate','Review examples of community work and storytelling','Confirm interest and availability; record shortlist reasons'],'Candidate shortlist'),
      phase('Meet and run a small trial','Use a structured conversation and a practical sample.',['Discuss responsibilities, support needs and availability','Agree a small practical sample and its evaluation criteria','Review the sample and collect feedback'],'Interview and trial notes'),
      phase('Approve people and terms','Confirm expectations, compensation and the decision owner.',['Prepare the proposed ambassador list and reasons','Confirm responsibilities, compensation and agreement terms','Obtain CEO sign-off before making commitments'],'Approved selection and terms',true),
      phase('Onboard and launch','Provide training, access, a first assignment and support.',['Introduce the programme lead and support contacts','Arrange agreed training and appropriate access after approval','Set a first assignment and regular check-ins'],'Onboarding and first assignment'),
      phase('Check results and improve','Review engagement, content quality and support needs.',['Review the agreed success measures and content quality','Ask ambassadors what support they need','Agree improvements and the next review'],'Programme review')
    ]};
    const businessPlan=guidePlan(prompt,['sales-pipeline','partnership-development','business-planning']);if(businessPlan)return businessPlan;
    if (/\b(event|workshop|exhibition|meetup)\b/i.test(prompt)) return {title:'Plan the event',intro:'From a clear brief to a well-run event and follow-up.',steps:[
      phase('Define the event','Agree the purpose, audience and format.',['Write the event brief','Confirm the intended audience and format','Agree the success measures'],'Event brief'),
      phase('Confirm people and place','Identify hosts, contributors and a suitable venue.',['Check relevant workspace activity','Confirm contributor interest and venue suitability','Document availability before choosing a date'],'People and venue shortlist'),
      phase('Build the programme','Make the schedule and practical needs clear.',['Draft the agenda','List equipment, accessibility and support needs','Confirm responsibilities'],'Programme and requirements'),
      phase('Approve commitments','Review fees and arrangements before committing.',['Collect quotes only where needed','Review proposed agreements and spend','Obtain the required approval'],'Approved arrangements',true),
      phase('Invite and prepare','Make the event easy to discover and attend.',['Prepare the invitation and registration flow','Share through relevant communities','Confirm attendance and the final checklist'],'Invitations and delivery checklist'),
      phase('Run the event','Coordinate delivery and support attendees.',['Brief the team','Run the agreed programme','Record issues and feedback'],'Event delivery'),
      phase('Follow up','Review outcomes and maintain connections.',['Thank contributors and attendees','Review the success measures','Agree the next steps'],'Follow-up and review')
    ]};
    if (/\b(content|campaign|social|video|footage)\b/i.test(prompt)) return {title:'Plan the content',intro:'A clear route from the brief to publication and learning.',steps:[
      phase('Define the brief','Agree the audience, message and outcome.',['Write the purpose and audience','Define the message and formats','Agree the success measures'],'Content brief'),
      phase('Develop the story','Use relevant people, sources and permissions.',['Identify suitable stories and contributors','Check source information','Confirm contributor consent and permissions'],'Story outline'),
      phase('Prepare production','Plan the people, materials and schedule.',['List the required assets and equipment','Confirm availability','Assign production responsibilities'],'Production plan'),
      phase('Create and edit','Produce the agreed content.',['Capture or develop the assets','Edit for the agreed formats','Check accuracy, captions and accessibility'],'Content draft'),
      phase('Review and approve','Obtain the appropriate review before publication.',['Collect feedback from the responsible people','Resolve changes','Confirm publication approval'],'Approved content',true),
      phase('Publish and learn','Release the content and review results.',['Confirm the publishing schedule','Publish through the approved channels','Review outcomes and improvements'],'Publication and review')
    ]};
    const operatingPlan=guidePlan(prompt);if(operatingPlan)return operatingPlan;
    return {title:text(prompt,100) || 'Your plan',intro:'Turn your goal into clear actions, owners and outcomes.',steps:[
      phase('Define the outcome','Make the goal and success criteria clear.',['Clarify the goal: '+text(prompt,250),'Agree what a successful outcome looks like','Identify missing information'],'Goal brief'),
      phase('Check the context','Use relevant records and dependencies.',['Review related work in the workspace','Confirm what is already underway','Identify dependencies and constraints'],'Context and dependencies'),
      phase('Agree the approach','Choose the practical route to the outcome.',['Compare suitable approaches','Confirm the people and resources needed','Document the chosen approach'],'Delivery approach'),
      phase('Assign the work','Make responsibilities and timing explicit.',['Assign an owner to each next step','Confirm availability before setting dates','Arrange the required support'],'Assigned work plan'),
      phase('Review and approve','Resolve decisions before commitments.',['Review the plan with the responsible people','Obtain any required spending or access approval','Confirm readiness to proceed'],'Reviewed plan'),
      phase('Deliver and review','Complete the work and check its impact.',['Carry out the agreed work','Review the success criteria','Document the outcome and remaining work'],'Outcome and follow-up')
    ]};
  }
  function contextFrom(prompt) {
    const count=prompt.match(/\b(?:recruit|hire|find|need|want|looking\s+for|lookig\s+for|seek)\s+(?:at least\s+|up to\s+)?(\d{1,4})\s+(?:new\s+|more\s+|additional\s+)?amb[a-z]*[ds][a-z]*r?s?\b/i)?.[1] || '';
    const region=text(prompt.match(/\b(?:in|from|across)\s+([^.,!?]+?)(?=\s+(?:by|with|starting|for|to)\s|[.,!?]|$)/i)?.[1],250);
    const candidate=prompt.match(/\b(?:by|start(?:ing)?(?:\s+on)?|from)\s+(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\b/i)?.[1] || '';
    let start='';try{if(candidate&&new Date(candidate+'T00:00:00Z').toISOString().slice(0,10)===candidate)start=candidate;}catch{}
    return {region,count,start};
  }
  async function api(path, data) {
    const options = {credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:{Accept:'application/json'}};
    if (data !== undefined) { options.method='POST'; options.headers['Content-Type']='application/json'; options.body=JSON.stringify(data); }
    const response=await fetch(ROOT+'api/workspace/'+path,options);
    const result=await response.json(); if(!response.ok) throw Error(result.error || 'The workspace could not complete this step.'); return result;
  }
  function adaptivePattern(result) {
    if (!result?.assisted || !text(result.summary) || !Array.isArray(result.sections)) throw Error('Cosmos could not generate a tailored response. Please try again.');
    const questions=[], steps=[];
    for (const section of result.sections.slice(0,8)) {
      if (/clarifying questions|questions(?: to| for| before|$)|missing information|details needed|before we proceed/i.test(section.title||'')) {
        questions.push(...(section.items?.length?section.items:[section.body]).flatMap(q=>String(q||'').split(/(?<=\?)\s+/)).map(q=>text(q.replace(/^\d+[.)]\s*/,''),600)).filter(Boolean));continue;
      }
      const items=(section.items||[]).map(a=>text(a,600)).filter(Boolean),output=items.find(x=>/^Output:\s*/i.test(x)),gate=items.find(x=>/^Gate:\s*/i.test(x));
      const actions=items.filter(x=>! /^(?:Output|Gate):\s*/i.test(x)).map(x=>x.replace(/^Action:\s*/i,''));
      if (!actions.length && text(section.body)) actions.push(text(section.body,1200));
      if (text(section.title) && actions.length) steps.push({...phase(text(section.title,140).replace(/^(?:Phase\s*\d+\s*[–—:-]|\d+[.)])\s*/i,''),text(section.body,1200),actions.slice(0,10),output?output.replace(/^Output:\s*/i,''):'Working notes for '+text(section.title,140),!!gate||/approval|sign.off|authoriz|review.*decision/i.test(section.title||'')),gate:gate?gate.replace(/^Gate:\s*/i,''):'',approvalLabel:gate?gate.replace(/^Gate:\s*/i,''):'Required review before commitments.'});
    }
    if (!steps.length) throw Error('Cosmos did not return actionable steps. Refine the question and try again.');
    return {title:text(result.title,100),intro:text(result.summary,1800),guideId:'adaptive',questions:questions.slice(0,6),steps,records:(Array.isArray(result.records)?result.records:[]).map(safeRecord).filter(Boolean).slice(0,8),incomplete:!!result.incomplete,checkedAt:result.checkedAt,adaptive:true};
  }
  async function generate(prompt, details='',overview) {
    const controller=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),45000);
    try {
      const context=processContext(prompt,overview),roster=context.people.map(p=>p.name+' — '+p.role).join('; ');
      const instructions='Build an inquiry-specific WORKSPACE PROCESS, not an essay. Each section is one phase: body explains purpose; items MUST include 2-4 separate Action: lines, one Output: proposed deliverable, and Gate: only where needed. Material missing facts belong in Clarifying questions. Use actual permitted recordIds for cards. No invented policy, prices, approval authority or completed actions.';
      const caution=context.kind==='personnel-review'?'Personnel REVIEW only. Ambassador is a recorded role, NOT proof of employment. First clarify arrangement, work-related reason, agreement/jurisdiction and who can decide; these are unknown. Never assume misconduct. Evidence is available ONLY if shown in actual live record fields; guidance/drafts do not prove a scorecard, coaching log, policy or HR team exists. Never invent notice periods, hours, days, improvement targets, mandatory hearings or required sign-offs. Suggest a fair conversation and support/role alternatives without fixed timeframes. Any transition is conditional on a confirmed authorised human and applicable professional advice. All outputs are PROPOSED drafts, not created/logged/approved records. Never decide dismissal, draft its notice, notify people or change access.':'';
      const supplied=text(details,1800),history=supplied?[0,900].filter(i=>i<supplied.length).map(i=>({role:'user',text:'Additional context supplied for this draft, not verified policy or approval:\n'+supplied.slice(i,i+900)})):[];
      const response=await fetch(ROOT+'api/cosmos',{method:'POST',credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},signal:controller.signal,body:JSON.stringify({page:location.pathname+location.search,history_owner:overview?.person?.id,history,prompt:text(['Goal: '+text(prompt,350),caution,instructions,roster?'Browser-visible roster (not authority or policy): '+roster:''].filter(Boolean).join('\n'),1950)})});
      const result=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw Error(result.error||'Cosmos could not generate this plan.');if(result.ownerId&&overview?.person?.id&&result.ownerId!==overview.person.id)throw Error('Your account changed. Refresh before preparing your own process.');const parsed=adaptivePattern(result),pattern=context.kind==='personnel-review'?reviewPattern(parsed):parsed;pattern.context=context;return pattern;
    } finally {clearTimeout(timer);}
  }
  function render(assistant,prompt,overview,details='') {
    if(assistant.dataset.cv3Generating==='yes')return assistant.querySelector('.cvgp-loading');assistant.dataset.cv3Generating='yes';assistant.setAttribute('aria-busy','true');const previous=assistant.querySelector('.cvgp');if(previous)previous.inert=true;
    const loading=el('section','cv3-plan cvgp'),message=el('p','cvgp-status','Cosmos is tailoring the response to your inquiry…');message.setAttribute('role','status');loading.append(message);assistant.append(loading);assistant.dataset.cv3Plan='yes';assistant.dataset.cv3PlanSource=overview?.person?.id?'live':'local';
    loading.classList.add('cvgp-loading');const retry=()=>{assistant.dataset.cv3Generating='yes';assistant.setAttribute('aria-busy','true');if(previous)previous.inert=true;loading.querySelector('button')?.remove();message.textContent='Cosmos is tailoring the response to your inquiry…';generate(prompt,details,overview).then(pattern=>{if(!loading.isConnected)return;delete assistant.dataset.cv3Generating;assistant.removeAttribute('aria-busy');pattern.refined=!!details;loading.remove();assistant.querySelectorAll('.cvgp').forEach(n=>n.remove());renderPlan(assistant,prompt,overview,pattern);}).catch(e=>{if(!loading.isConnected)return;delete assistant.dataset.cv3Generating;assistant.removeAttribute('aria-busy');if(previous)previous.inert=false;message.textContent=e.name==='AbortError'?'Cosmos took too long. Your inquiry is preserved; try again.':e.message;loading.append(button('Try again',retry));});};retry();return loading;
  }
  function renderPlan(assistant, prompt, overview, pattern) {
    const profile=overview?.person,team=overview?.team || [],context=pattern.context||processContext(prompt,overview),isPersonnel=context.kind==='personnel-review';if(!isPersonnel&&meetingGoal(prompt))pattern.guideId='meeting-planning';
    if(isPersonnel){pattern.guideId='personnel-review';pattern.title='Review '+(context.subject?.name||'the working arrangement');pattern.intro='This is a draft review process, not a dismissal decision. Confirm the working arrangement, the concern and who can decide before considering any change. General guidance is not company policy or legal advice.';}
    const storageKey=profile?.id ? 'cv-goal-plan:v1:'+encodeURIComponent(profile.id)+':'+encodeURIComponent(prompt.toLowerCase()) : '';
    let model={version:1,processVersion:PROCESS_RELEASE,answers:{},choice:'',goal:prompt,title:pattern.title,region:'',count:'',start:'',budget:'',currency:'AED',costNotes:'',project:'',steps:pattern.steps,submission:null,...(recruitment(prompt)?contextFrom(prompt):{})};
    try {
      const saved=storageKey && JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(storageKey));
      if(saved?.processVersion===PROCESS_RELEASE&&saved.goal?.toLowerCase()===prompt.toLowerCase()&&!saved.submission){model.answers={...saved.answers};model.choice=text(saved.choice,100);}
      if(!pattern.refined && saved?.version===1 && saved.aiGenerated && (saved.processVersion===PROCESS_RELEASE||saved.submission)&& saved.goal?.toLowerCase()===prompt.toLowerCase() && Array.isArray(saved.steps) && saved.steps.length>0 && saved.steps.length<=20 && saved.steps.every(s=>uuid(s.id)&&text(s.title,140)&&Array.isArray(s.actions)&&s.actions.length<=10)) {
        model={...model,...saved};
        model.steps=model.steps.map(s=>({...s,owner:team.some(p=>p.id===s.owner)?s.owner:'',title:text(s.title,140),summary:text(s.summary,1200),output:text(s.output,250),actions:s.actions.map(a=>({label:text(a.label,1200),done:!!a.done})),approval:!!s.approval,approvalLabel:text(s.approvalLabel,250)}));
        if(model.submission && (!uuid(model.submission.requestId)||!Array.isArray(model.submission.tasks)||model.submission.tasks.some(t=>!uuid(t.requestId)))) model.submission=null;
      }
    } catch { /* Unreadable local drafts never affect workspace authorization. */ }
    model.aiGenerated=true;let busy=false,reviewing=false;
    const board=el('section','cv3-plan cvgp');board.setAttribute('aria-label','Cosmos plan');board.dataset.cv3Touched='no';
    assistant.classList.add('cvgp-assistant');
    const grid=el('div','cvgp-grid'),main=el('section','cvgp-main'),side=el('aside','cvgp-side');
    const header=el('div','cvgp-heading'),title=el('h2','',model.title),badge=el('span','cvgp-badge','Draft');
    const close=button('Close plan',()=>{const box=assistant.closest('#cv-ws-search-results');if(box)box.hidden=true;document.querySelector('#cv-ws-search')?.focus();});close.className='cvgp-icon';close.setAttribute('aria-label','Close plan');close.title='Close plan';close.replaceChildren(glyph('close'));
    header.append(title,badge,close);
    const goal=el('div','cvgp-goal'),goalCopy=el('p','',prompt),editGoal=button('Edit goal',()=>{const input=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-search');if(input){input.value=model.goal;input.focus();input.select();}});goal.append(goalCopy,editGoal);
    main.append(header,goal,el('p','cvgp-intro',pattern.intro));
    const contextCards=el('div','cvgp-context-cards');contextCards.setAttribute('aria-label','Process context');
    function contextCard(label,title,copy){const card=el('article','cvgp-context-card');card.append(el('p','cvgp-kicker',label),el('h3','',title),el('p','',copy));contextCards.append(card);return card;}
    const intent=contextCard('Recognised process',isPersonnel?'Working arrangement review':context.kind==='meeting-planning'?'Meeting coordination':context.kind==='recruitment'?'Ambassador recruitment':'Inquiry-specific process','Draft guidance · no actions executed.');
    intent.append(button('Review what is needed',()=>{side.querySelector('input,textarea,select')?.focus();side.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'auto'})}));
    for(const person of context.people){const card=contextCard('Person in your inquiry',person.name,person.role+' · from your workspace roster');const more=el('details');more.append(el('summary','','View role context'),el('p','',isPersonnel?'A role title does not establish employment, an agreement, wrongdoing or dismissal authority.':'Confirm their involvement and availability before assigning work.'));card.append(more);}
    if(isPersonnel){const card=contextCard('Decision gate','Human review required','No change is approved. The authorised decision-maker and applicable agreement must be confirmed.');card.append(button('Explore alternatives',()=>{choices.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});choices.querySelector('button')?.focus()}));}
    main.append(contextCards);
    const sectionHead=el('div','cvgp-section-heading');sectionHead.append(el('h3','','Your process'));const count=el('span','cvgp-muted');sectionHead.append(count);
    const accordionName='cvgp-'+crypto.randomUUID();const steps=el('div','cvgp-steps'),addStep=button('Add a phase',()=>{if(model.steps.length>=20)return;model.steps.push(phase('New phase','Describe what needs to happen.',['Add the first action'],'Expected outcome'));changed();paintSteps(model.steps.length-1);});
    main.append(sectionHead,steps,addStep);
    const review=el('section','cvgp-review');review.hidden=true;review.setAttribute('aria-label','Review plan');
    const foot=el('div','cvgp-footer'),status=el('p','cvgp-status','Draft only — no tasks created yet.');status.setAttribute('role','status');status.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
    const isMeeting=!isPersonnel&&pattern.guideId==='meeting-planning';
    if(isMeeting)board.classList.add('is-meeting');
    if(isMeeting)status.textContent='Draft plan · no meeting scheduled or invitations sent.';
    if(isPersonnel){board.classList.add('is-personnel');status.textContent='Review draft only · no staff change, notice, task or notification created.';}
    const actions=el('div','cvgp-actions'),save=button('Save draft',()=>persist(true)),next=button(isPersonnel?'Prepare review brief':isMeeting?'Review task plan':'Review plan',isPersonnel?prepareReviewBrief:showReview,!isMeeting);actions.append(save,next);foot.append(status,actions);
    if(isMeeting)actions.append(button('Prepare invitation',()=>{
      const trigger=document.querySelector('[data-ws-meeting-new]');if(!trigger){status.textContent='Open Schedule a meeting to prepare your invitation.';return;}
      trigger.click();const form=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-dialog form[data-ws-enhanced="meeting"]');if(!form){status.textContent='The invitation form could not open. Try Schedule a meeting.';return;}
      form.elements.title.value=pattern.title==='Plan your ambassador meeting'?'Ambassador meeting':'Team meeting';
      form.elements.location.value=text(model.region,240);
      const agenda=model.steps.find(s=>/agenda/i.test(s.title))||blueprint(prompt).steps[3];
      form.elements.note.value=['Purpose: '+text(model.goal,500),'Proposed agenda:',...agenda.actions.map(a=>'- '+a.label)].join('\n').slice(0,2000);
      status.textContent='Invitation draft opened. Choose attendees and times, then review before sending.';
      form.elements.title.focus();
    },true));
    function changed(){board.dataset.cv3Touched='yes';status.textContent=isPersonnel?'Unsaved draft · no staff changes or notices sent.':'Unsaved changes · no tasks created yet.';}
    function prepareReviewBrief(){
      if(!validate())return;const trigger=document.querySelector('[data-ws-create-request]');if(!trigger){status.textContent='Open Make a request to prepare a confidential review brief.';return;}trigger.click();const form=document.querySelector('#cv-ws-request-form');if(!form){status.textContent='The request form could not open. Your process is preserved.';return;}
      form.elements.title.value=text('Review working arrangement: '+(context.subject?.name||model.goal),140);
      form.elements.body.value=text(['DRAFT REVIEW BRIEF — not a dismissal instruction or an approved decision.','User inquiry for review: '+model.goal,...questions.map(([key,label])=>label+': '+(text(model.answers?.[key],275)||'Not confirmed')),model.choice?'Route to review: '+model.choice:'Outcome: not decided','Review process:',...model.steps.map((s,i)=>(i+1)+'. '+s.title+' — '+s.output),'Confirm the correct reviewer and who can access this sensitive request before submitting. No notice, agreement, access or staff role should change without authorised human review.'].join('\n'),2500);
      if(form.elements.kind){form.elements.kind.value='other';form.elements.kind.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));}status.textContent='Brief prepared in the native request form. Check its audience and reviewer before submitting; nothing has been sent.';form.elements.title.focus();
    }
    function persist(announce=false){if(!storageKey){status.textContent='Wait for your workspace account to load, then try again.';return false;}try{model.savedAt=Date.now();localStorage.setItem(storageKey,JSON.stringify(model));const keys=[];for(let i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){const key=localStorage.key(i);if(key?.startsWith('cv-goal-plan:v1:'+encodeURIComponent(profile.id)+':'))keys.push(key);}if(keys.length>20){keys.filter(k=>k!==storageKey).sort((a,b)=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(a)).savedAt-JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(b)).savedAt;}catch{return 0;}}).slice(0,keys.length-20).forEach(k=>localStorage.removeItem(k));}if(announce)status.textContent='Draft saved in this browser · no new tasks created.';return true;}catch{status.textContent='This browser could not save the draft. Keep this page open and try again.';return false;}}
    function labeled(label,value,change,{type='text',placeholder='Not set',max=250,min,step}={}){const wrap=el('label','cvgp-field',label),input=el('input');input.type=type;input.value=value || '';input.placeholder=placeholder;input.maxLength=max;if(min!==undefined)input.min=min;if(step)input.step=step;input.addEventListener('input',()=>{change(input.value);changed();});wrap.append(input);return {wrap,input};}
    function ownerSelect(step){const wrap=el('label','cvgp-field','Owner'),select=el('select');select.setAttribute('aria-label','Owner for '+step.title);select.append(new Option('Not assigned',''));team.forEach(p=>select.append(new Option(p.name,p.id)));select.value=step.owner;select.addEventListener('change',()=>{step.owner=select.value;changed();});wrap.append(select);return wrap;}
    function paintSteps(openIndex=0){steps.replaceChildren();count.textContent=model.steps.length+' phases';model.steps.forEach((step,index)=>{
      const detail=el('details','cvgp-step');detail.setAttribute('name',accordionName);detail.open=index===openIndex;const summary=el('summary'),num=el('span','cvgp-number',String(index+1)),copy=el('span','cvgp-summary-copy');copy.append(el('strong','',step.title),el('span','',step.summary));summary.append(num,copy,el('span','cvgp-state',isPersonnel?/transition|offboard|notice|implement|terminate/i.test(step.title)?'Conditional':'Review draft':step.gate?'Needs review':'Draft'),glyph('chevron'));detail.append(summary);
      const content=el('div','cvgp-step-content'),list=el('div','cvgp-checklist');if(index)content.append(el('p','cvgp-dependency','Previous step: '+model.steps[index-1].title+'. Confirm its outcome before proceeding.'));if(isPersonnel)content.append(el('p','cvgp-muted','Draft checklist only. Checking an item does not approve a decision or change a staff record.'));step.actions.forEach(action=>{const label=el('label'),check=el('input');check.type='checkbox';check.checked=action.done;check.addEventListener('change',()=>{action.done=check.checked;changed();});label.append(check,el('span','',action.label));list.append(label);});content.append(list);
      const fields=el('div','cvgp-step-fields');fields.hidden=isMeeting||isPersonnel;fields.append(ownerSelect(step),labeled('Due date (optional)',step.due,v=>step.due=v,{type:'date'}).wrap);content.append(fields,el('p','cvgp-output','Output: '+step.output));if(step.approval)content.append(el('p','cvgp-approval','Decision gate: '+(step.gate||step.approvalLabel||'Confirm the required reviewer before commitments.')));
      const edit=button('Edit phase',()=>{editor.hidden=!editor.hidden;if(!editor.hidden)editor.querySelector('input')?.focus();}),editor=el('div','cvgp-editor');editor.hidden=true;
      const phaseTitle=labeled('Phase title',step.title,v=>{step.title=v;copy.querySelector('strong').textContent=v;},{max:140});const description=labeled('Summary',step.summary,v=>{step.summary=v;copy.querySelector('span').textContent=v;},{max:500});const actionField=el('label','cvgp-field','Actions (one per line)'),area=el('textarea');area.rows=4;area.value=step.actions.map(a=>a.label).join('\n');area.maxLength=2000;area.addEventListener('input',()=>{step.actions=area.value.split('\n').filter(s=>s.trim()).slice(0,10).map(label=>({label:text(label,500),done:false}));changed();});actionField.append(area);editor.append(phaseTitle.wrap,description.wrap,actionField,labeled('Expected output',step.output,v=>{step.output=v;content.querySelector('.cvgp-output').textContent='Output: '+v;}).wrap,button('Done editing',()=>paintSteps(index)));
      const controls=el('div','cvgp-phase-actions'),remove=button('Delete phase',()=>{if(model.steps.length===1){status.textContent='Keep at least one phase in the plan.';return;}model.steps.splice(index,1);changed();paintSteps(Math.min(index,model.steps.length-1));});remove.className='cvgp-icon';remove.setAttribute('aria-label','Delete phase '+step.title);remove.title='Delete phase';remove.replaceChildren(glyph('trash'));controls.append(edit,remove);content.append(editor,controls);detail.append(content);
      steps.append(detail);
    });}
    side.append(el('h3','',isPersonnel?'Before any decision':'Make the process yours'),el('p','cvgp-muted',isPersonnel?'Confirm the arrangement and the review route. Do not enter protected traits or unrelated personal details.':'Answer the relevant questions, then let Cosmos refine the steps.'));
    const questions=isPersonnel?[
      ['workingArrangement','Working arrangement','Employee, contractor, ambassador agreement, or not yet confirmed'],
      ['concern','What prompted this review?','Describe the work-related concern without assuming wrongdoing'],
      ['agreement','Agreement and jurisdiction','Which agreement and applicable jurisdiction need professional review?'],
      ['authority','Who can decide?','Confirm the authorised reviewer; a role title alone is not approval']
    ]:(pattern.questions||[]).slice(0,4).map((q,i)=>['question'+i,q,'Your answer or what is still unknown']);
    for(const [key,label,hint]of questions){const field=labeled(label,model.answers?.[key]||'',value=>model.answers[key]=value,{max:275,placeholder:hint});side.append(field.wrap);}
    if(isPersonnel&&context.coordinator)side.append(el('p','cvgp-muted','Recorded operations contact: '+context.coordinator.name+(context.decisionContact?' · Recorded CEO: '+context.decisionContact.name:'')+'. These are possible review contacts, not a confirmed decision or automatic notification.'));
    const choices=el('section','cvgp-choices');if(isPersonnel){choices.append(el('h3','','Choose what to explore'),el('p','cvgp-muted','Choosing a route only changes this draft.'));for(const choice of ['Support or role adjustment','A reviewed transition']){const pick=button(choice,()=>{model.choice=choice;for(const b of choices.querySelectorAll('button'))b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b===pick));changed()});pick.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(model.choice===choice));choices.append(pick);}side.append(choices);}
    const extra=el('label','cvgp-field','Additional context'),extraInput=el('textarea');extraInput.rows=3;extraInput.maxLength=200;extraInput.placeholder='Anything else Cosmos should consider?';extra.append(extraInput);side.append(extra,button('Refine process',()=>{const details=text([...Object.entries(model.answers||{}).filter(([,v])=>text(v)).map(([k,v])=>text(questions.find(q=>q[0]===k)?.[1]||k,100)+': '+text(v,275)),model.choice?'Explore: '+model.choice:'',text(extraInput.value,200)].filter(Boolean).join('\n'),1800);if(!details||assistant.dataset.cv3Generating==='yes')return;if(board.dataset.cv3Touched==='yes'&&!persist())return;render(assistant,prompt,overview,details);}));
    const audienceContext=['sales-pipeline','partnership-development','business-planning'].includes(pattern.guideId)||pattern.title==='Plan the content';const showHeadcount=!audienceContext;
    if(!pattern.adaptive||isMeeting)side.append(labeled(recruitment(prompt)?'Where should we recruit?':isMeeting?'Restaurant or location (optional)':audienceContext?'Which market or audience?':pattern.guideId==='artist-discovery'?'Which craft or region?':'Where will this happen?',model.region,v=>model.region=v).wrap);
    if(showHeadcount&&!pattern.adaptive)side.append(labeled(recruitment(prompt)?'How many ambassadors?':pattern.guideId==='artist-discovery'?'How many artists? (optional)':'How many people? (optional)',model.count,v=>model.count=v,{type:'number',min:'1',step:'1'}).wrap);
    if(!isPersonnel)side.append(labeled(recruitment(prompt)?'When should they start?':'Target date (optional)',model.start,v=>model.start=v,{type:'date'}).wrap);
    const costs=el('details','cvgp-costs'),costTitle=el('summary','', 'Costs (optional)');costs.hidden=pattern.adaptive&&!/\b(cost|budget|spend|purchase|buy|fund|quote|price)\b/i.test(prompt);costTitle.append(glyph('chevron'));costs.append(costTitle,el('p','cvgp-muted','Add only if funding is needed.'));
    const currency=el('label','cvgp-field','Currency'),currencySelect=el('select');['AED','USD','EUR','GBP','COP','PHP','SAR'].forEach(c=>currencySelect.append(new Option(c,c)));currencySelect.value=model.currency;currencySelect.addEventListener('change',()=>{model.currency=currencySelect.value;changed();});currency.append(currencySelect);costs.append(labeled('Budget (optional)',model.budget,v=>model.budget=v,{type:'number',min:'0',step:'0.01'}).wrap,currency,labeled('What needs funding?',model.costNotes,v=>model.costNotes=v,{max:500}).wrap,el('p','cvgp-muted','An estimate does not approve spending.'));side.append(costs);
    const related=el('details','cvgp-related');related.open=!!pattern.records?.length;related.append(el('summary','','Relevant workspace cards'),el('p','cvgp-muted','Open the actual record to check its latest detail.'));
    const recordCards=el('div','cvgp-records');for(const record of pattern.records||[]){const card=el('article','cvgp-record-card');card.append(el('p','cvgp-kicker',record.kind||'Workspace record'),el('h3','',record.title));if(record.description)card.append(el('p','',record.description));if(record.status)card.append(el('p','cvgp-muted','Recorded status: '+record.status));const link=el('a','cvgp-action','Open record');link.href=record.url;card.append(link);recordCards.append(card);}related.append(recordCards);
    const relevant=(overview?.tasks||[]).filter(t=>{const s=(t.title+' '+t.description).toLowerCase();return !['done','archived'].includes(t.status)&&(recruitment(prompt)?/recruit|scorecard|ambassador programme|ambassador program|new ambassador/.test(s):prompt.toLowerCase().split(/\W+/).filter(w=>w.length>4).some(w=>s.includes(w)));}).slice(0,4);
    for(const task of relevant.filter(t=>!pattern.records?.some(r=>r.id===t.id))){const a=el('a','cvgp-action',task.title);a.href=ROOT+'workspace/?task='+encodeURIComponent(task.id);related.append(a);}if(!relevant.length&&!pattern.records?.length)related.append(el('p','cvgp-muted',profile?'No matching open work found in your available records.':'Workspace context is loading.'));if(pattern.incomplete)related.append(el('p','cvgp-muted','Some permitted context could not be checked; missing facts remain unconfirmed.'));side.append(related);
    const guidance=el('details','cvgp-guidance');guidance.append(el('summary','','Planning guidance'),el('p','cvgp-muted','General guidance is separate from company documents and live work.'));
    const guidanceBody=el('div');guidance.append(guidanceBody);side.append(guidance);let guidanceState='idle';
    guidance.addEventListener('toggle',async()=>{
      if(!guidance.open||guidanceState!=='idle')return;guidanceState='loading';guidanceBody.replaceChildren(el('p','cvgp-muted','Checking the guidance available to your account…'));
      const controller=new AbortController(),timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),10000);
      try{
        const response=await fetch(ROOT+'api/knowledge/query?q='+encodeURIComponent(prompt)+'&domain=business',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:{Accept:'application/json'},signal:controller.signal});
        if(!response.ok)throw Error('Guidance could not be checked. Open this section again to retry.');const result=await response.json(),k=result.knowledge;
        if(!k||!Array.isArray(k.guidance)||!Array.isArray(k.documents))throw Error('Guidance could not be checked.');guidanceBody.replaceChildren();
        for(const g of k.guidance.slice(0,3)){const item=el('section');item.append(el('h3','',text(g.title,140)),el('p','',text(g.summary,600)),el('p','cvgp-muted','General operating guidance · not company policy or a live record'));const list=el('ul');for(const q of (g.questions||[]).slice(0,4))list.append(el('li','',text(q,300)));item.append(list);guidanceBody.append(item);}
        if(k.documents.length){guidanceBody.append(el('h3','','Company references'));for(const d of k.documents.slice(0,3)){const item=el('p','cvgp-context',text(d.title,160)+' · '+text(d.status,60)+' · '+text(d.source_date||'Undated',40)+'\n'+text(d.content,600));guidanceBody.append(item);}}
        if(!k.guidance.length&&!k.documents.length)guidanceBody.append(el('p','cvgp-muted','No matching guidance or company references were found in your permitted sources.'));
        if(k.unavailable?.length)guidanceBody.append(el('p','cvgp-muted','Some live records could not be checked. No missing facts were assumed.'));guidanceState='ready';
      }catch(e){guidanceState='idle';guidanceBody.replaceChildren(el('p','cvgp-muted',e.name==='AbortError'?'Guidance took too long. Open this section again to retry.':e.message));}finally{clearTimeout(timeout)}
    });
    const answer=text(assistant.querySelector('.cv-assistant-answer')?.textContent);if(answer){const advice=el('details','cvgp-related');advice.append(el('summary','','Assistant context'),el('p','cvgp-context',answer));side.append(advice);}
    function validate(){if(!profile?.id){status.textContent='Your workspace account is still loading.';return false;}if(!model.steps.every(s=>text(s.title,140)&&s.actions.length)){status.textContent='Give every phase a title and at least one action.';return false;}if(model.count!==''&&(!Number.isInteger(Number(model.count))||Number(model.count)<1)){status.textContent='Enter a whole number of people greater than zero.';return false;}if(model.budget!==''&&(!Number.isFinite(Number(model.budget))||Number(model.budget)<0)){status.textContent='Enter a valid budget or leave it unset.';return false;}return true;}
    function showReview(){if(!validate())return;reviewing=true;main.hidden=true;side.hidden=true;review.hidden=false;review.replaceChildren();const heading=el('h2','','Review your plan');heading.tabIndex=-1;review.append(heading,el('p','cvgp-muted','Creating this plan records one task per phase. Assigned owners may receive workspace notifications. Spending and ambassador selection still need the required approval.'));
      const assignment=el('div','cvgp-review-assignment');assignment.append(el('p','','Choose an owner for every phase.'),button('Assign unassigned phases to me',()=>{model.steps.forEach(s=>{if(!s.owner)s.owner=profile.id;});changed();paintSteps();showReview();}));review.append(assignment);
      const projectLabel=el('label','cvgp-field','Project'),projectSelect=el('select');projectSelect.append(new Option('Create a new project',''));(overview?.projects||[]).forEach(p=>projectSelect.append(new Option(p.title,p.id)));projectSelect.value=model.project;projectSelect.disabled=!!model.submission;projectSelect.addEventListener('change',()=>{model.project=projectSelect.value;changed();});projectLabel.append(projectSelect);review.append(projectLabel);
      for(const step of model.steps){const row=el('div','cvgp-review-row');row.append(el('strong','',step.title),ownerSelect(step),el('span','cvgp-muted',step.due?'Due '+step.due:'No due date'));if(step.approval)row.append(el('span','cvgp-approval',step.approvalLabel?'Required approval':'CEO sign-off'));review.append(row);}
      const missing=pattern.adaptive?[]:[!model.region&&(audienceContext?'market or audience':'region'),showHeadcount&&!model.count&&'headcount',!model.start&&'start date'].filter(Boolean);if(missing.length)review.append(el('p','cvgp-muted','Still not set: '+missing.join(', ')+'. These can be confirmed in the brief.'));
      const back=button('Back to plan',()=>{reviewing=false;review.hidden=true;main.hidden=false;side.hidden=false;next.textContent='Review plan';paintSteps();});back.classList.add('cvgp-back');back.disabled=!!model.submission;review.append(back);
      next.textContent=model.submission?'Resume creating tasks':'Create '+model.steps.length+' tasks';status.textContent=model.submission?'Resume the saved creation attempt. Existing tasks will not be duplicated.':'Review owners and details before creating work.';heading.focus();
    }
    if(!isPersonnel){next.removeEventListener('click',showReview);next.addEventListener('click',()=>reviewing?create():showReview());}
    function description(step){return [step.summary,'Actions:',...step.actions.map(a=>'- '+a.label),'Output: '+step.output,'Goal: '+model.goal,model.region?'Region: '+model.region:'',model.count?'Target headcount: '+model.count:'',model.start?'Target start: '+model.start:'',model.budget!==''?'Optional budget estimate: '+model.currency+' '+model.budget:'',model.costNotes?'Funding notes: '+model.costNotes:'',step.approval?(step.approvalLabel||'CEO sign-off required before commitments.') : ''].filter(Boolean).join('\n').slice(0,2500);}
    async function create(){if(isPersonnel){status.textContent='Prepare a review brief instead. This process cannot execute a personnel decision.';return;}if(busy||!validate())return;if(model.steps.some(s=>!team.some(p=>p.id===s.owner))){status.textContent='Choose an owner for every phase before creating tasks.';return;}
      busy=true;for(const control of board.querySelectorAll('button,input,select,textarea'))control.disabled=true;
      try{
        if(!model.submission){if(model.project){const existing=await api('projects?id='+encodeURIComponent(model.project));const allowed=new Set([existing.project.owner_id,...existing.members,...team.filter(p=>['ceo','operations'].includes(p.role)).map(p=>p.id)]);if(model.steps.some(s=>!allowed.has(s.owner)))throw Error('An assigned owner is not a member of this project. Choose another owner or create a new project.');}
          model.submission={requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),projectId:model.project || '',tasks:model.steps.map(s=>({requestId:s.id,title:s.title,description:description(s),owner_id:s.owner,due_date:s.due,status:'todo',priority:'normal',approval_required:s.approval,resultId:''}))};if(!persist())return;
        }
        if(!persist())return;const job=model.submission;if(!job.projectId){status.textContent='Creating the project…';const p=await api('projects',{title:text(model.title,120),description:text(model.goal,1800),due_date:'',members:[...new Set(job.tasks.map(t=>t.owner_id))],requestId:job.requestId});if(!uuid(p.id))throw Error('The project response could not be verified. Retry this saved attempt.');job.projectId=p.id;if(!persist())return;}
        for(let i=0;i<job.tasks.length;i++){const task=job.tasks[i];if(task.resultId)continue;status.textContent='Creating task '+(i+1)+' of '+job.tasks.length+'…';const result=await api('tasks',{...task,project_id:job.projectId});if(!uuid(result.id))throw Error('The task response could not be verified. Retry this saved attempt.');task.resultId=result.id;if(!persist())return;}
        job.complete=true;persist();status.textContent=job.tasks.length+' tasks created. Open the project to continue.';actions.replaceChildren();const open=el('a','cvgp-action is-primary','Open project');open.href=ROOT+'workspace/?view=projects&project='+encodeURIComponent(job.projectId);actions.append(open);review.replaceChildren(el('h2','','Your plan is now in Work'),el('p','cvgp-muted','The project and tasks are recorded. Required approvals remain in place.'));window.__cvExperienceV3?.invalidateOverview?.();
      }catch(e){status.textContent=e.message;next.textContent=model.submission?'Resume creating tasks':'Create '+model.steps.length+' tasks';}finally{busy=false;for(const control of board.querySelectorAll('button,input,select,textarea'))control.disabled=false;if(model.submission&&!model.submission.complete){for(const control of board.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea'))control.disabled=true;review.querySelector('.cvgp-review-assignment')?.remove();const back=review.querySelector('.cvgp-back');if(back)back.disabled=true;save.disabled=true;} }
    }
    paintSteps();grid.append(main,side,review);board.append(grid,foot);const form=assistant.querySelector('form');if(form)form.before(board);else assistant.append(board);
    assistant.dataset.cv3Plan='yes';assistant.dataset.cv3PlanSource=profile?.id?'live':'local';
    if(model.submission?.complete){reviewing=true;main.hidden=true;side.hidden=true;review.hidden=false;review.append(el('h2','','This plan has already been created'));actions.replaceChildren();const open=el('a','cvgp-action is-primary','Open project');open.href=ROOT+'workspace/?view=projects&project='+encodeURIComponent(model.submission.projectId);actions.append(open);status.textContent='Your saved plan is already in Work.';}
    else if(model.submission){showReview();for(const input of review.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea'))input.disabled=true;review.querySelector('.cvgp-review-assignment')?.remove();save.disabled=true;}
    else if(model.savedAt)status.textContent='Saved draft restored from this browser.';
    return board;
  }
  window.__cvGoalPlanner={release:PROCESS_RELEASE,recruitment,meetingGoal,personnelGoal,processContext,reviewPattern,shouldPlan,blueprint,contextFrom,adaptivePattern,generate,render};
})();

(()=>{'use strict';
if(window.__cvExperienceV3)return;

const ROOT='/communiverse/';
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s),$$=s=>[...document.querySelectorAll(s)];
const safe=v=>typeof v==='string'?v.trim():'';
const escapeText=(v,n=250)=>safe(v).slice(0,n);
const validID=v=>/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$/.test(safe(v));
const node=(name,cls,text)=>{const e=document.createElement(name);if(cls)e.className=cls;if(text!==undefined)e.textContent=escapeText(String(text),1500);return e};
const roles={ceo:'CEO',operations:'Operations',ambassador:'Ambassador','ambassador-lead':'Ambassador lead',ai:'AI & artist programme',brand:'Brand',platform:'Platform',latam:'LatAm'};
const labels={blocked:'Blocked',review:'In review',todo:'To do',doing:'In progress',done:'Completed'};
const avatarURL=p=>{const photo=safe(p?.photo||p?.photo_url);if(photo.startsWith(ROOT))return photo;const slug=safe(p?.profile_slug);return validID(slug)?ROOT+'_public/avatar/'+encodeURIComponent(slug):''};
let teamData=null,teamPending=null,teamExpiry=0;
async function authorizedOverview(){if(teamData&&Date.now()<teamExpiry)return teamData;if(teamPending)return teamPending;
 teamPending=(async()=>{const res=await fetch(ROOT+'api/workspace/overview',{credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',headers:{Accept:'application/json'}});if(!res.ok)throw Error('Workspace authorization required');const data=await res.json();if(!Array.isArray(data.team)||!Array.isArray(data.tasks)||!Array.isArray(data.projects))throw Error('Workspace response unavailable');teamData=data;teamExpiry=Date.now()+35000;return data;})().finally(()=>{teamPending=null});return teamPending;
}
function personInfo(id,data){if(!validID(id))return {id:'',name:'Unassigned'};return data?.team?.find(p=>p.id===id)||data?.person?.id===id&&data.person||{id,name:id};}
function circle(person,{relationship='',small=false}={}){
 const name=escapeText(person?.name||person?.id||'Unassigned',120),wrap=node('span','cv3-person-circle'+(small?' cv3-person-circle-sm':''));
 wrap.dataset.cv3Tooltip=name+(relationship?' · '+relationship:'');wrap.title=wrap.dataset.cv3Tooltip;wrap.setAttribute('aria-label',wrap.dataset.cv3Tooltip);
 wrap.append(node('span','cv3-person-initial',name.slice(0,1).toUpperCase()));
 const url=avatarURL(person);
 if(url){const img=node('img');img.src=url;img.alt='';img.loading='lazy';img.decoding='async';img.addEventListener('error',()=>img.remove(),{once:true});wrap.append(img)}
 return wrap;
}

function eligibleFromSelect(select,overview){return [...select.options].filter(opt=>opt.value&& !opt.disabled).map(opt=>({id:opt.value,label:opt.textContent.trim(),person:personInfo(opt.value,overview)}));}
function updatePickerValue(picker,select,options){
 const value=select.value;for(const button of picker.querySelectorAll('[data-cv3-person-id]')){const on=button.dataset.cv3PersonId===value;button.setAttribute('aria-checked',String(on));button.classList.toggle('is-selected',on)}
 if(picker.querySelector('.cv3-picker-count'))picker.querySelector('.cv3-picker-count').textContent=options.length+' eligible';
}
function createPersonPicker(select,overview){
 if(select.dataset.cv3Picker||select.multiple)return;
 const options=eligibleFromSelect(select,overview);if(!options.length)return;
 const root=node('div','cv3-person-picker');root.setAttribute('role','radiogroup');root.setAttribute('aria-label',select.closest('label')?.childNodes[0]?.textContent?.trim()||'Select responsible person');

 const grid=node('div','cv3-person-grid');for(const item of options){const b=node('button','cv3-person-choice');b.type='button';b.dataset.cv3PersonId=item.id;b.setAttribute('role','radio');b.title=item.label;b.append(circle(item.person,{relationship:roles[item.person.role]||item.label}));b.append(node('span','cv3-person-name',item.person.name||item.label));if(item.person.role)b.append(node('small','cv3-person-role',roles[item.person.role]||item.person.role));
 b.addEventListener('click',()=>{select.value=item.id;select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));updatePickerValue(root,select,options)});grid.append(b)}root.append(grid);
 if(search)search.addEventListener('input',()=>{const q=search.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();for(const btn of grid.children)btn.hidden=!!q&&!btn.textContent.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(q)});
 select.dataset.cv3Picker='yes';select.classList.add('cv3-native-hidden');select.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');select.tabIndex=-1;
 select.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',root);select.addEventListener('change',()=>updatePickerValue(root,select,options));updatePickerValue(root,select,options);
}

const kindHints={resource:'General support',asset:'Equipment or an asset',kit:'Camera, tools or supplies',subscription:'Software and renewals',training:'Skills and guidance',access:'Access and permissions',pay:'Compensation review',contract:'Agreement or contract',travel:'Travel support',ambassador:'Ambassador programme',other:'Other support'};

function renderRoute(root,overview,select,amount){const list=kindReviewers(overview,select.value,safe(amount?.value||''));root.replaceChildren();const heading=node('p','cv3-label','Expected approval journey');const chain=node('div','cv3-approval-timeline');chain.setAttribute('aria-label','Expected approval sequence');for(const [i,id] of list.entries()){const info=personInfo(id,overview);const part=node('div','cv3-timeline-person');part.append(circle(info,{relationship:i===0?'First reviewer':'Next stage'}),node('span','',info.name||id));if(i)chain.append(node('span','cv3-timeline-line',''));chain.append(part)}root.append(heading,chain,node('p','cv3-note','The reviewer is determined by Communiverse policy on submission. This preview cannot change permissions.'))}

function upgradeTaskForm(){const form=$('#cv-ws-dialog #cv-ws-task-form');if(!form||form.dataset.cv3Action)return;form.dataset.cv3Action='yes';const state=form.querySelector('select[name=status]');if(!state)return;const title=form.querySelector('input[name=title]')?.value||'Task';const prior=node('section','cv3-task-actions');prior.append(node('p','cv3-label','Next useful step'));

 function suggested(label,value){const button=node('button','cv3-quiet-button',label);button.type='button';button.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(value&&![...state.options].some(o=>o.value===value&&!o.disabled))return;if(value){state.value=value;state.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));note.textContent='Ready to save. }
function upgradeFocus(){const content=$('#cv-ws-content'),app=$('#cv-ws-app');if(!content||!app||app.hidden)return;
 const active=$('.cv-ws-tabs [data-ws-tab=overview][aria-pressed=true]');if(!active||content.querySelector('.cv3-focus-board'))return;
 authorizedOverview().then(data=>{if(!content.isConnected||!$('.cv-ws-tabs [data-ws-tab=overview][aria-pressed=true]')||content.querySelector('.cv3-focus-board'))return;
 const items=data.tasks.filter(t=>['blocked','review','todo','doing'].includes(t.status));items.sort((a,b)=>({blocked:0,review:1,doing:2,todo:3}[a.status]??5)-({blocked:0,review:1,doing:2,todo:3}[b.status]??5)||String(a.due_date||'9999').localeCompare(String(b.due_date||'9999')));
 const approvals=data.requests.filter(r=>r.reviewer_id===data.person?.id&&['keiffer','approval','operations','needs-info'].includes(r.stage));const panel=node('section','cv3-focus-board');panel.append(node('p','cv3-kicker','YOUR FOCUS'));
 const top=node('div','cv3-focus-head');top.append(node('h2','','What needs attention'));top.append(node('p','cv3-note',items.filter(x=>x.status==='blocked').length+' blocked · '+items.filter(x=>x.status==='review').length+' under review · '+approvals.length+' approvals waiting with you'));panel.append(top);
 const grid=node('div','cv3-focus-grid');const projects=new Map(data.projects.map(x=>[x.id,x]));
 for(const t of items.slice(0,5)){const card=node('article','cv3-focus-item');const top=node('div','cv3-card-top');top.append(node('span','cv3-state cv3-state-'+t.status,labels[t.status]||t.status));top.append(node('small','cv3-project',projects.get(t.project_id)?.title||'Project'));card.append(top,node('h3','',t.title||'Task'));
 const line=node('div','cv3-card-bottom');const owner=personInfo(t.owner_id,data);line.append(circle(owner,{relationship:'Task owner',small:true}),node('span','cv3-note',owner.name||'Unassigned'));
 const open=node('button','cv3-action',t.status==='blocked'?'Resolve blocker':t.status==='review'?'Open review':'Open task');open.type='button';open.dataset.wsTask=t.id;open.title='Open task: '+t.title;line.append(open);card.append(line);grid.append(card)}
 for(const req of approvals.slice(0,2)){const card=node('article','cv3-focus-item cv3-focus-request');card.append(node('span','cv3-state','Your decision'),node('h3','',req.title||'Request'));const line=node('div','cv3-card-bottom');line.append(circle(personInfo(req.created_by,data),{relationship:'Requester',small:true}));const open=node('button','cv3-action','Review request');open.type='button';open.dataset.wsRequest=req.id;line.append(open);card.append(line);grid.append(card)}
 if(!grid.children.length)grid.append(node('p','cv3-note','Your work is up to date. New priorities will appear here.'));
 panel.append(grid);content.insertAdjacentElement('afterbegin',panel);
 }).catch(()=>{});
}
function mentionRoot(select){const anchor=select.closest('label')||select;const root=anchor.nextElementSibling;return root?.classList.contains('cv3-mention-picker')?root:null}
function personForMention(opt,overview){const info=personInfo(opt.value,overview);const label=opt.textContent.trim();const name=info.name&&info.name!==info.id?info.name:label;return {...info,id:opt.value,name:name||label||opt.value}}
function syncMentionPicker(picker,select){for(const button of picker.querySelectorAll('[data-cv3-person-id]')){const opt=[...select.options].find(o=>o.value===button.dataset.cv3PersonId);const on=!!opt?.selected;button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(on));button.classList.toggle('is-selected',on)}
 const n=[...select.selectedOptions].length;const count=picker.querySelector('.cv3-mention-count');if(count)count.textContent=n===0?'None selected':n===1?'1 person selected':n+' people selected'}
function createMentionPicker(select,overview){if(select.dataset.cv3Mentions==='yes')return;
 const items=[...select.options].filter(opt=>opt.value&&!opt.disabled).map(opt=>({id:opt.value,label:opt.textContent.trim(),person:personForMention(opt,overview)}));
 if(!items.length){delete select.dataset.cv3Mentions;return}
 const root=node('div','cv3-mention-picker');root.setAttribute('role','group');root.setAttribute('aria-label','Notify people');
 let search=null;if(items.length>8){search=node('input','cv3-picker-search');search.type='search';search.placeholder='Find a person';search.setAttribute('aria-label','Filter people to notify');root.append(search)}
 const row=node('div','cv3-mention-row');
 for(const item of items){const b=node('button','cv3-mention-choice');b.type='button';b.dataset.cv3PersonId=item.id;b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');b.title=item.person.name||item.label;b.append(circle(item.person,{relationship:''}));b.append(node('span','cv3-person-name',item.person.name||item.label));
  b.addEventListener('click',()=>{const opt=[...select.options].find(o=>o.value===item.id);if(!opt)return;opt.selected=!opt.selected;select.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}));syncMentionPicker(root,select)});row.append(b)}
 root.append(row);const count=node('p','cv3-mention-count','');count.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');root.append(count);
 if(search)search.addEventListener('input',()=>{const q=search.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();for(const btn of row.children)btn.hidden=!!q&&!btn.textContent.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(q)});
 select.dataset.cv3Mentions='yes';select.classList.add('cv3-native-hidden');select.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');select.tabIndex=-1;
 (select.closest('label')||select).insertAdjacentElement('afterend',root);select.addEventListener('change',()=>syncMentionPicker(root,select));syncMentionPicker(root,select);
}
function refreshMentionFaces(select,overview){const picker=mentionRoot(select);if(!picker)return;for(const button of picker.querySelectorAll('[data-cv3-person-id]')){const opt=[...select.options].find(o=>o.value===button.dataset.cv3PersonId);if(!opt)continue;const person=personForMention(opt,overview);const face=button.querySelector('.cv3-person-circle');const name=button.querySelector('.cv3-person-name');if(name)name.textContent=person.name;button.title=person.name;if(face)face.replaceWith(circle(person,{relationship:''}))}}
function upgradeMentionSelects(){for(const select of $$('#cv-ws-dialog #cv-ws-comment-form select[name=mentions][multiple]')){if(select.dataset.cv3Mentions==='yes'||select.dataset.cv3Mentions==='pending')continue;select.dataset.cv3Mentions='pending';
 const paint=data=>{if(select.isConnected&&select.dataset.cv3Mentions!=='yes')createMentionPicker(select,data)};
 paint(teamData||{team:[]});
 authorizedOverview().then(data=>{if(!select.isConnected)return;if(select.dataset.cv3Mentions==='yes')refreshMentionFaces(select,data);else paint(data)}).catch(()=>{if(select.isConnected&&select.dataset.cv3Mentions!=='yes')paint({team:[]})});
}}
const planWords=new Set(['plan','this','that','with','from','your','have','need','trip','days','what','mind','want','please','make','into','about','the','and','for','order','ordering','camera','cameras','kit','kits','ambassador','ambassadors']);
const planStop=new Set('a an the to of for in on at and or with our their them they this that these those want order ordering cameras camera kits kit days day budget trip travel plan please need item items'.split(' '));
const placeNames={uae:'UAE',colombia:'Colombia',philippines:'Philippines',saudi:'Saudi Arabia',pakistan:'Pakistan',malaysia:'Malaysia',india:'India',peru:'Peru',china:'China',uzbekistan:'Uzbekistan',latam:'LatAm'};
function editDistance(a,b){const m=[];for(let i=0;i<=a.length;i++)m[i]=[i];for(let j=0;j<=b.length;j++)m[0][j]=j;for(let i=1;i<=a.length;i++)for(let j=1;j<=b.length;j++){const cost=a[i-1]===b[j-1]?0:1;m[i][j]=Math.min(m[i-1][j]+1,m[i][j-1]+1,m[i-1][j-1]+cost)}return m[a.length][b.length]}
function closeWord(word,target){word=String(word||'').toLowerCase();target=String(target||'').toLowerCase();if(!word||!target||word===target)return word===target;if(word.length<4||target.length<4||Math.abs(word.length-target.length)>2)return false;return editDistance(word,target)<=(Math.max(word.length,target.length)>=8?2:1)}
function promptWords(prompt){return String(prompt||'').toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/).filter(Boolean)}
function mentionsWord(prompt,targets){const words=promptWords(prompt);return targets.some(target=>words.some(word=>word===target||closeWord(word,target)))}
function planKind(prompt){if(window.__cvGoalPlanner?.recruitment(prompt))return 'recruit';if(mentionsWord(prompt,['camera','cameras','camra','kit','kits','gear','equipment','lens']))return 'kit';if(mentionsWord(prompt,['trip','travel','flight','itinerary'])||/\bvisit\b/i.test(prompt))return 'travel';if(/\b(budget|cost|calculate|how much|estimate)\b/i.test(prompt))return 'budget';return 'plan'}
function readQuantity(prompt){const m=String(prompt||'').match(/\b(\d{1,4})\s+([a-z]{3,})/i);if(!m)return null;const word=m[2].toLowerCase();if(planStop.has(word)&&!closeWord(word,'camera')&&!closeWord(word,'cameras'))return null;if(closeWord(word,'camera')||closeWord(word,'cameras')||closeWord(word,'kit')||closeWord(word,'kits')||['items','item','units','packs'].includes(word))return Number(m[1]);return null}
function placeLabel(code){const key=safe(code).toLowerCase();return placeNames[key]||(key?key.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+key.slice(1):'')}
function personPlace(person){return placeLabel(person?.region)}
function readDays(prompt){const m=prompt.match(/\b(\d{1,3})\s*days?\b/i);return m?Number(m[1]):null}
function readBudget(prompt){const m=prompt.match(/(?:usd|\$)\s*(\d{1,7}(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?)|\b(\d{1,7}(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?)\s*(?:usd|dollars)\b/i);if(!m)return null;const n=Number(String(m[1]||m[2]).replace(/,/g,''));return Number.isFinite(n)?n:null}
function money(n){const v=Number(n);return Number.isFinite(v)?v.toLocaleString(undefined,{maximumFractionDigits:2}):'0'}
function namedPeople(prompt,overview){const team=overview?.team||[],found=[],seen=new Set(),words=promptWords(prompt).filter(word=>!planStop.has(word));
 function add(p){const key=p?.id||p?.name;if(!p||!key||seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);found.push(p)}
 if(words.some(word=>word.endsWith('s')&&(word==='ambassadors'||closeWord(word,'ambassadors')))){for(const p of team)if(p.role==='ambassador')add(p)}
 for(const p of team){const first=safe(p?.name).split(/\s+/)[0];if(first.length<3)continue;if(words.some(word=>word===first.toLowerCase()||closeWord(word,first)))add(p)}
 return found}
function routePeople(assistant,overview){const team=overview?.team||[];return [...assistant.querySelectorAll('.cv-assistant-route span')].map(s=>s.textContent.replace(/^\s*\d+\.\s*/,'').trim()).filter(Boolean).map(name=>{const person=team.find(p=>safe(p.name).toLowerCase()===name.toLowerCase());return person||{id:'',name}})}
function relatedWork(prompt,overview){const words=prompt.toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/).filter(w=>w.length>3&&!planWords.has(w));return (overview?.tasks||[]).filter(t=>{const blob=[t.title,t.description,t.owner_id,t.created_by].map(v=>safe(v).toLowerCase()).join(' ');return words.some(w=>blob.includes(w))}).slice(0,3)}
function planTitle(prompt,people,kind){const qty=readQuantity(prompt);if(kind==='kit'&&people.length)return (qty?qty+' ':'')+'cameras for the ambassadors';if(kind==='travel')return people[0]?.name?'Trip plan for '+people[0].name:'Trip plan';if(kind==='kit')return qty?qty+' cameras':'Kit plan';if(kind==='budget')return 'Cost plan';return people[0]?.name?'Plan with '+people[0].name:'A plan from your question'}
function planSteps(kind,prompt,people,related,overview){const who=people.map(p=>p.name).filter(Boolean).join(', ')||'the people you add';const qty=readQuantity(prompt);const lead=(overview?.team||[]).find(p=>p.role==='ambassador-lead');const places=[...new Set(people.map(personPlace).filter(Boolean))];if(kind==='travel')return [['Purpose',prompt],['Who travels',who+' is on this draft. Tap a circle to leave someone out.'],['Dates','Set the number of days. The total follows that figure.'],['Money','The budget you named is split across the days and people. Change any figure.'],['Review','The approval circles are the expected reviewers. The draft below is what can be recorded.'],['After approval','Book travel only after the request is approved, then share the outcome with the people on this plan.']];if(kind==='kit')return [['Name the item',qty?qty+' cameras. Change the quantity if the number is wrong.':prompt],['Who receives one',who+(people.length&&people.every(p=>p.role==='ambassador')?' — these are the ambassadors recorded on the team.':' — tap a circle to change who is included.')],['Check the split',people.length&&qty?(qty+' cameras across '+people.length+' ambassadors'+(qty%people.length===0?' is '+(qty/people.length)+' each if split evenly.':' does not split evenly. Decide who receives more than one.')):'Count the people, then decide whether this is shared stock.'],['Say what is in each kit','Camera body, lens, memory card, strap and case. Cross out anything you are not ordering.'],['Price one camera','Type the unit cost. The total is quantity times that price. No daily rate is used.'],['Choose the place',places.length?'Recorded places: '+places.join(', ')+'. These are the regions on the people, not a demographic study.':'No region is recorded on these people yet. Ask which city the cameras are for.'],['Ask locally','Cultural and demographic notes are not stored on these profiles. Ask each ambassador what matters in their city before anything ships.'],[lead?'Confirm with '+lead.name:'Confirm the list',lead?lead.name+' is the ambassador lead. Ask them to confirm who is included.':'Check the ambassador list with the programme lead.'],['Send it for review','The approval circles are the expected reviewers. Submitting the draft is what records the request.'],['Hand the cameras over','After approval, write who collects each camera, which city it goes to, and when it comes back.']];const open=(overview?.tasks||[]).filter(t=>['blocked','review','todo','doing'].includes(t.status)).slice(0,4);return [['What you asked',prompt],['Who is involved',who],['Already in the workspace',open.length?open.map(t=>t.title).filter(Boolean).join(', '):'No open task matches this yet.'],['The next small move','Rewrite this into the first thing someone can do today.'],['Who decides','The approval circles are who Cosmos expects to review it. Submitting the draft is what asks them.'],['What gets recorded','Keep the draft below in step with this plan before you submit.']]}
function cosmosFacts(prompt,overview,kind,people){const team=overview?.team||[],facts=[];const ambassadors=team.filter(p=>p.role==='ambassador');const lead=team.find(p=>p.role==='ambassador-lead');const aboutAmbassadors=mentionsWord(prompt,['ambassador','ambassadors'])||people.some(p=>p.role==='ambassador');if(aboutAmbassadors){facts.push(['Ambassadors',ambassadors.length?ambassadors.map(p=>p.name+(personPlace(p)?' · '+personPlace(p):'')).join(', '):'No ambassador is recorded on this team yet.']);if(lead)facts.push(['Ambassador lead',lead.name+' confirms who is in the programme.']);facts.push(['The role','An ambassador discovers makers, tells the story with their permission, brings people together, and stays connected.'])}const places=[...new Set((aboutAmbassadors?ambassadors:people).map(personPlace).filter(Boolean))];if(places.length)facts.push(['Places on record',places.join(', ')+'. These are regions stored on people, not a demographic study.']);if(kind==='kit'||kind==='travel')facts.push(['Estimates','Equipment and travel figures stay estimates until the draft is submitted and reviewed.']);const blocked=(overview?.tasks||[]).filter(t=>t.status==='blocked').slice(0,3);if(blocked.length&&(kind==='plan'||mentionsWord(prompt,['task','tasks','blocked','work'])))facts.push(['Blocked work',blocked.map(t=>t.title).filter(Boolean).join(', ')]);return facts.slice(0,5)}
function upgradeMind(){const assistant=$('#cv-ws-search-results .cv-ws-assistant');if(!assistant||assistant.dataset.cv3Plan==='yes'||assistant.dataset.cv3Plan==='pending')return;if(!assistant.querySelector('.cv-assistant-answer'))return;const prompt=safe($('#cv-ws-search')?.value);if(!prompt)return;assistant.dataset.cv3Plan='pending';

 paint(teamData||null);

Ask again to prepare the plan.';});
}
function drawMind(assistant,prompt,overview){const kind=planKind(prompt);if(window.__cvGoalPlanner&&!['kit','travel'].includes(kind)){const original=assistant.querySelector('form[data-assistant-submit]');const explicitGoal=/\b(plan\w*|organis\w*|organiz\w*|launch\w*|build\w*|develop\w*|improve\w*|prepar\w*|train\w*|onboard\w*)\b/i.test(prompt);if(!window.__cvGoalPlanner.shouldPlan(prompt)||(original&&!original.querySelector('[name=project_id]')&&!explicitGoal&&kind!=='recruit')){assistant.dataset.cv3Plan='yes';return;}if(!overview?.person){assistant.classList.add('cvgp-pending');if(!assistant.querySelector('.cvgp-pending-note')){const note=node('p','cvgp-pending-note','Preparing your plan from your workspace…');note.setAttribute('role','status');assistant.append(note);}assistant.dataset.cv3PlanSource='local';return;}assistant.classList.remove('cvgp-pending');assistant.querySelector('.cvgp-pending-note')?.remove();window.__cvGoalPlanner.render(assistant,prompt,overview);return;}const travelers=namedPeople(prompt,overview),assumedDays=readDays(prompt)==null,days=readDays(prompt)||(kind==='travel'?3:1),budget=readBudget(prompt),related=relatedWork(prompt,overview);
 const reviewers=routePeople(assistant,overview);const chain=reviewers.length?reviewers:kindReviewers(overview,kind==='kit'?'kit':'travel',budget??'').map(id=>personInfo(id,overview));
 const subject=travelers.length?travelers:(overview?.person?[overview.person]:[]);
