 const head=node('div','cv3-plan-head');head.append(node('p','cv3-kicker','COSMOS'));head.append(node('h3','',planTitle(prompt,subject,kind)));head.append(node('p','cv3-note','Cosmos prepared this from your question and the records already in the workspace. Tap a person, rewrite a step, or change a figure.'));const knows=node('div','cv3-cosmos-knows');knows.setAttribute('aria-label','What Cosmos knows');const known=cosmosFacts(prompt,overview,kind,subject);if(known.length){knows.append(node('p','cv3-label','What Cosmos knows'));for(const [title,detail] of known){const fact=node('article','cv3-cosmos-fact');fact.append(node('strong','',title),node('span','',detail));knows.append(fact)}}board.append(head,knows);
 const peopleBlock=node('div','cv3-plan-people');
 const travelerChain=node('div','cv3-plan-chain cv3-plan-travelers');travelerChain.setAttribute('role','group');travelerChain.setAttribute('aria-label','People on this plan');
 const reviewChain=node('div','cv3-plan-chain cv3-plan-reviewers');reviewChain.setAttribute('role','group');reviewChain.setAttribute('aria-label','Expected reviewers');
 function link(){return node('span','cv3-plan-link','')}
 function paintLinks(row){const kids=[...row.children];for(let i=0;i<kids.length;i++){if(!kids[i].classList.contains('cv3-plan-link'))continue;const prev=kids[i-1],next=kids[i+1];const on=prev?.getAttribute('aria-pressed')!=='false'&&next?.getAttribute('aria-pressed')!=='false';kids[i].classList.toggle('is-dim',!on)}}
 function personNode(person,relationship,onToggle){const item=node('button','cv3-plan-person');item.type='button';item.dataset.cv3PersonId=person.id||person.name;item.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');item.append(circle(person,{relationship}),node('span','cv3-person-name',person.name||'Person'),node('small','cv3-person-role',relationship));
  function paint(){const on=item.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true';item.setAttribute('aria-label',(person.name||'Person')+' · '+relationship+(on?' · included':' · left out'))}
  item.addEventListener('click',()=>{item.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(item.getAttribute('aria-pressed')!=='true'));board.dataset.cv3Touched='yes';paint();onToggle();});paint();return item}
 function selectedCount(row){return row.querySelectorAll('.cv3-plan-person[aria-pressed="true"]').length}
 const steps=node('ol','cv3-plan-steps');const progress=node('p','cv3-plan-progress','');
 function renumber(){[...steps.querySelectorAll('.cv3-plan-num')].forEach((n,i)=>{n.textContent=String(i+1)})}
 function appendStep(title,detail,focus){const li=node('li','cv3-plan-step');const btn=node('button','cv3-plan-step-btn');btn.type='button';btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');btn.append(node('span','cv3-plan-num',String(steps.children.length+1)));btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Mark this step done');
  const copy=node('div','cv3-plan-copy');const titleInput=node('input','cv3-plan-step-title');titleInput.value=title;titleInput.setAttribute('aria-label','Step title');const detailInput=node('textarea','cv3-plan-step-detail');detailInput.value=detail;detailInput.rows=2;detailInput.setAttribute('aria-label','Step detail');

  copy.append(titleInput,detailInput,remove);li.append(btn,copy);steps.append(li);if(focus)titleInput.focus();return li}
 const meter=node('div','cv3-plan-meter');meter.setAttribute('role','progressbar');meter.setAttribute('aria-label','Steps ready');const meterBar=node('span','cv3-plan-meter-bar');meter.append(meterBar);

 function syncProgress(){const all=steps.querySelectorAll('.cv3-plan-step-btn'),on=[...all].filter(b=>b.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true').length,total=Math.max(1,all.length);progress.textContent=on+' of '+all.length+' steps ready';meterBar.style.width=Math.round(on/total*100)+'%';meter.setAttribute('aria-valuenow',String(on));meter.setAttribute('aria-valuemax',String(all.length));paintStages();paintMail();paintReady()}
 function paintStages(){const names=kind==='kit'?['People','Price','Review','Submit']:['People','Dates','Review','Submit'];const peopleOn=selectedCount(travelerChain)>0;const priced=kind==='kit'?num(unitField.input,0)>0:num(dayField.input,1)>0;const reviewed=selectedCount(reviewChain)>0;let now=0;if(peopleOn)now=1;if(peopleOn&&priced)now=2;if(peopleOn&&priced&&reviewed)now=3;stages.replaceChildren();names.forEach((name,i)=>{const chip=node('span','cv3-plan-stage',name);if(i<now)chip.classList.add('is-done');if(i===now)chip.classList.add('is-now');stages.append(chip)})}
 function paintMail(){const seen=new Set();mailList.replaceChildren();let waiting=0;function addRow(btn,why){const name=btn.querySelector('.cv3-person-name')?.textContent||'Person';const key=name+'|'+why;if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);const on=btn.getAttribute('aria-pressed')==='true';const line=node('div','cv3-plan-mail-row'+(on?'':' is-off'));const copy=node('div','cv3-plan-mail-copy');copy.append(node('strong','',name),node('small','',why));const pill=node('span','cv3-plan-mail-pill',on?'Not sent yet':'No email');line.append(node('span','cv3-plan-mail-dot'),copy,pill);mailList.append(line);if(on)waiting++}
  for(const btn of travelerChain.querySelectorAll('.cv3-plan-person'))addRow(btn,kind==='kit'?'Receives the update':'On the trip');
  for(const btn of reviewChain.querySelectorAll('.cv3-plan-person'))addRow(btn,'Asked to review');
  mailSummary.textContent=waiting+(waiting===1?' person':' people')+' will be emailed when you submit · 0 sent'}
 function paintReady(){const checks=kind==='kit'?[['Quantity is set',num(qtyField.input,0)>0],['Someone is included',selectedCount(travelerChain)>0],['Unit cost is added',num(unitField.input,0)>0]]:[['Days are set',num(dayField.input,0)>0],['Someone is included',selectedCount(travelerChain)>0]];checks.push(['A reviewer is included',selectedCount(reviewChain)>0]);readyList.replaceChildren();for(const [label,ok] of checks){const li=node('li','cv3-plan-ready-item'+(ok?' is-done':''),(ok?'Ready · ':'Still open · ')+label);readyList.append(li)}}
 const calc=node('div','cv3-plan-calc');calc.setAttribute('aria-label','Working estimate');

 const qty=readQuantity(prompt);const dayField=field('cv3-plan-days','Days',String(days),'1'),peopleField=field('cv3-plan-people-count',kind==='kit'?'Recipients': 'People',String(Math.max(1,subject.length||1)),'1'),dailyField=field('cv3-plan-daily','Daily allowance',kind==='kit'?'0':(budget&&days?String(Math.round((budget/days/Math.max(1,subject.length))*100)/100):'0'),'0.01'),budgetField=field('cv3-plan-budget','Budget',budget==null?'':String(budget),'0.01'),qtyField=field('cv3-plan-qty','Quantity',String(qty||1),'1'),unitField=field('cv3-plan-unit','Unit cost','','0.01');

 const peopleInput=peopleField.input;const formula=node('p','cv3-plan-formula','');const total=node('p','cv3-plan-total','');const balance=node('p','cv3-plan-balance','');
 const amount=assistant.querySelector('input[name=amount]');let amountEdited=false;amount?.addEventListener('input',e=>{if(e.isTrusted)amountEdited=true});
 function num(input,fallback){const n=Number(input.value);return Number.isFinite(n)&&n>=0?n:fallback}
 function syncPeople(){if(peopleInput.dataset.cv3Edited==='yes')return;peopleInput.value=String(Math.max(1,selectedCount(travelerChain)))}
 function recalc(){syncPeople();let estimate=0;if(kind==='kit'){const q=Math.max(1,num(qtyField.input,1)),unit=num(unitField.input,0),n=Math.max(1,selectedCount(travelerChain));estimate=q*unit;formula.textContent=q+' × '+money(unit)+' = '+money(estimate);total.textContent='Working estimate '+money(estimate);balance.textContent=q+' across '+n+' '+(n===1?'person':'people')+(q%n===0?' · '+(q/n)+' each if split evenly':' · does not split evenly');}else{const d=Math.max(1,num(dayField.input,1)),p=Math.max(1,num(peopleInput,1)),daily=num(dailyField.input,0),cap=budgetField.input.value===''?null:num(budgetField.input,0);estimate=d*p*daily;formula.textContent=d+' × '+p+' × '+money(daily)+' = '+money(estimate);total.textContent='Working estimate '+money(estimate);if(cap==null)balance.textContent='Add a budget to see what remains.';else balance.textContent=(estimate>cap?'Over the named budget by ':'Remaining in the named budget: ')+money(Math.abs(cap-estimate))}total.dataset.cv3Estimate=String(Math.round(estimate*100)/100);if(amount&&!amountEdited)amount.value=estimate?String(Math.round(estimate*100)/100):'';syncProgress();paintLinks(travelerChain);paintLinks(reviewChain)}
 function onTraveler(){recalc()}

 const places=[...new Map(subject.filter(p=>personPlace(p)).map(p=>[personPlace(p),p])).keys()];

 const recs=node('div','cv3-plan-recs');recs.append(node('p','cv3-label','Recommendations'));
 const answer=assistant.querySelector('.cv-assistant-answer')?.textContent?.trim();if(answer)recs.append(node('article','cv3-plan-rec',answer));

// Clear recruitment and meeting goals use the planning flow immediately. Asking
// does not create an assistant request draft, project, task or notification.
async function settleWorkspace(){const out=$('#cv-ws-status');if(!out||!/Loading.*workspace/i.test(out.textContent))return;await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let timer;const observer=new MutationObserver(check);function finish(){clearTimeout(timer);observer.disconnect();resolve();}function check(){if(!/Loading.*workspace/i.test(out.textContent))finish();}observer.observe(out,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true});timer=setTimeout(()=>{observer.disconnect();reject(Error('Workspace is still loading'));},20000);check();});}
async function directPlan(prompt){const box=$('#cv-ws-search-results');if(!box)return;const assistant=node('section','cv-ws-assistant');const notice=node('p','','Checking your workspace context…');notice.setAttribute('role','status');assistant.append(notice);box.hidden=false;box.replaceChildren(assistant);try{const overview=await authorizedOverview();await settleWorkspace();if(!assistant.isConnected)return;box.hidden=false;notice.remove();window.__cvGoalPlanner.render(assistant,prompt,overview);}catch(e){if(!assistant.isConnected)return;notice.textContent='Your workspace context did not load. Please try again.';const retry=node('button','cv3-quiet-button','Try again');retry.type='button';retry.addEventListener('click',()=>{void directPlan(prompt)});assistant.append(retry);}}
function interceptPlan(event){const input=$('#cv-ws-search');const fromButton=event.type==='click'&&event.target.closest?.('button[data-ws-ai-search]');const fromEnter=event.type==='keydown'&&event.target===input&&event.key==='Enter'&&!event.isComposing;const planner=window.__cvGoalPlanner;if(!input||(!fromButton&&!fromEnter)||!planner?.shouldPlan(input.value))return;event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();void directPlan(input.value.trim());}
document.addEventListener('click',interceptPlan,true);document.addEventListener('keydown',interceptPlan,true);

}
function updateFileUI(){for(const input of $$('.cv-workspace input[type=file]')){if(input.dataset.cv3File)return;input.dataset.cv3File='yes';const form=input.closest('form');if(!form)return;form.classList.add('cv3-upload-form');const caption=form.querySelector('.cvfix-file-caption');if(caption)caption.textContent='Choose a reference · 20 MB images/documents or 32 MB video';}}

function boot(){run();observer.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot,{once:true});else boot();
})();


;
/* Communiverse calls: no SDK, media request, external asset or token on mount.
 * api(path, options) receives a relative v4 path and JSON string bodies.
 * Parent should call the returned refresh() when its calls refresh event arrives.
 * Parent should await unmount() when switching conversations where possible.
 *
 * Future enablement only (keep current strict self CSP while disabled):
 * script-src https://cdn.jsdelivr.net (Core ESM, UI loader + UI modules below)
 * connect-src https://api.realtime.cloudflare.com
 *   https://api-silos.realtime.cloudflare.com https://da-collector.realtime.cloudflare.com
 *   https://location.realtime.cloudflare.com https://r2.cloudflarestorage.com
 *   https://rtk-assets.realtime.cloudflare.com https://rtk-uploads.realtime.cloudflare.com
 *   wss://socket-edge.realtime.cloudflare.com
 * media-src blob: https://rtk-assets.realtime.cloudflare.com
 *   https://rtk-uploads.realtime.cloudflare.com
 * img-src data: blob: https://rtk-assets.realtime.cloudflare.com
 *   https://rtk-uploads.realtime.cloudflare.com (plus existing member-photo sources).
 * The UI Kit creates inline/shadow styles: review a scoped style-src-elem/style-src-attr
 * policy or a self-hosted SDK build before enabling; do not relax production now.
 * Parent serves CALLS_CSS at CALLS_STYLES_PATH exported from calls.mjs. The
 * disabled UI requests only that same-origin stylesheet; no inline style needed.
 * WebRTC additionally needs STUN/TURN network ports (not a CSP directive).
 * No provider plugins, remote fonts, frames, example meetings or SDK precall tests.
 * Sources checked 2026-10-12:
 * https://developers.cloudflare.com/realtime/realtimekit/ui-kit/
 * https://developers.cloudflare.com/realtime/realtimekit/ui-kit/state-management/
 * https://developers.cloudflare.com/realtime/realtimekit/ui-kit/branding/design-system/
 * https://developers.cloudflare.com/realtime/realtimekit/network-allowlist/
 */
(function () {
  'use strict';
  // Both versions verified from the official npm registry on 2026-10-12.
  const CORE_URL = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@cloudflare/realtimekit@2.0.3/dist/index.es.js';
  const UI_URL = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@cloudflare/realtimekit-ui@2.0.3/loader/index.es2017.js';
  const DESIGN_URL = 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@cloudflare/realtimekit-ui@2.0.3/dist/index.js';
  let mounted = null;
  const STYLES_URL = '/communiverse/__cvconnect_v4_calls.css';
  function installStyles() {
    if (document.getElementById('cv4-calls-style')) return;
    const style = document.createElement('link'); style.id = 'cv4-calls-style';
    style.rel = 'stylesheet'; style.href = STYLES_URL; document.head.append(style);
  }
  function el(tag, className, text) {
    const node = document.createElement(tag);
    if (className) node.className = className;
    if (text !== undefined) node.textContent = String(text);
    return node;
  }
  const iconPaths = {
    audio:'M8 3h8v12H8z M5 10v3a7 7 0 0 0 14 0v-3 M12 20v2 M8 22h8',
    video:'M3 6h12v12H3z M15 10l6-4v12l-6-4',
    huddle:'M8 4a3 3 0 1 0 0 6 3 3 0 0 0 0-6 M16 5a2 2 0 1 0 0 4 2 2 0 0 0 0-4 M2 20v-3a6 6 0 0 1 12 0v3 M16 12a5 5 0 0 1 6 5v3',
    leave:'M9 5H4v14h5 M10 12h12 M18 8l4 4-4 4'
  };
  function icon(kind) {
    const node = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','svg');
    node.setAttribute('viewBox','0 0 24 24'); node.setAttribute('fill','none'); node.setAttribute('stroke','currentColor');
    node.setAttribute('stroke-width','1.7'); node.setAttribute('stroke-linecap','round'); node.setAttribute('stroke-linejoin','round'); node.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    const path = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg','path'); path.setAttribute('d',iconPaths[kind] || iconPaths.audio); node.append(path); return node;
  }
  function button(label,kind,handler,disabled,className) {
    const node = el('button','cv4-calls-button' + (className ? ' ' + className : ''));
    node.type = 'button'; node.disabled = Boolean(disabled); node.append(icon(kind),el('span','',label));
    node.addEventListener('click', () => { if (!node.disabled) Promise.resolve().then(handler).catch(() => {}); });
    return node;
  }
  const kindLabel = kind => kind === 'video' ? 'Video call' : kind === 'huddle' ? 'Huddle' : 'Voice call';
  function explain(error, fallback) {
    // Parent/API errors can contain arbitrary text; display via textContent only.
    return error && typeof error.reason === 'string' ? error.reason : fallback;
  }
  async function importSDK() {
    const [core,ui,design] = await Promise.all([import(CORE_URL),import(UI_URL),import(DESIGN_URL)]);
    ui.defineCustomElements();
    await customElements.whenDefined('rtk-meeting');
    return {Client:core.default, provideDesignSystem:design.provideRtkDesignSystem};
  }
  async function dataFrom(result) {
    if (result && typeof result.json === 'function') {
      const data = await result.json();
      if (!result.ok) throw data;
      return data;
    }
    if (result && result.error) throw result;
    return result;
  }

  function mount(host, {conversation,api,loadSDK = importSDK} = {}) {
    if (!host || typeof host.replaceChildren !== 'function' || typeof api !== 'function') throw new TypeError('Calls need a host and the Connect API.');
    if (mounted) mounted.destroy().catch(() => {});
    installStyles();
    const conversationId = typeof conversation === 'string' ? conversation : conversation && conversation.id;
    const people = conversation && conversation.participants || [];
    const root = el('section','cv4-calls'); root.setAttribute('aria-label','Conversation calls');
    const card = el('div','cv4-calls-card'); const area = el('div','cv4-calls-meeting'); area.hidden = true;
    const notice = el('p','cv4-calls-notice'); notice.setAttribute('role','status'); notice.setAttribute('aria-live','polite'); notice.hidden = true;
    root.append(card,notice,area); host.replaceChildren(root);
    let disposed = false, enabled = false, busy = false, active = null, meeting = null, view = null;
    let reason = 'Checking call availability…', task = null, leaving = null, timer = null, statusSequence = 0;
    let ownsParticipant = false, pendingCallId = null;
    const aborter = new AbortController();
    async function call(path,options) { return dataFrom(await api(path,options)); }
    const post = (path,body,options = {}) => call('calls/' + path, {method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({conversation_id:conversationId,...body}),...options});
    const notify = text => { notice.textContent = text || ''; notice.hidden = !text; };
    function render() {
      if (disposed) return;
      const heading = el('div','cv4-calls-heading'); const orb = el('span','cv4-calls-orb'); orb.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      heading.append(orb,el('h3','cv4-calls-title',active ? kindLabel(active.kind) : 'Talk together'));
      const copy = el('p','cv4-calls-copy',enabled ? (meeting ? 'You are in this conversation’s call.' : active ?
        (active.state === 'active' ? 'A private call is ready for the people in this conversation.' : 'The call is finishing or waiting for a previous request.') :
        'Start a voice call, video call or a casual audio huddle.') : reason);
      const actions = el('div','cv4-calls-actions'); actions.setAttribute('aria-label','Call actions');
      if (!active) {
        for (const [kind,label] of [['audio','Voice'],['video','Video'],['huddle','Huddle']]) {
          const control = button(label,kind,() => start(kind),!enabled || busy);
          if (!enabled) control.title = reason;
          actions.append(control);
        }
      } else {
        if (!meeting) actions.append(button('Join ' + (active.kind === 'huddle' ? 'huddle' : 'call'),active.kind,join,!enabled || busy || active.state !== 'active','cv4-calls-button-primary'));
        if (meeting || ownsParticipant || active.joined) actions.append(button('Leave','leave',() => leave(),busy,'cv4-calls-button-danger'));
        if (active.can_end) actions.append(button('End for everyone','leave',end,!enabled || busy,'cv4-calls-button-danger'));
      }
      if (!enabled && !busy) actions.append(button('Check again','audio',refresh,false));
      card.replaceChildren(heading,copy);
      if (active && active.participants && active.participants.length) {
        const avatars = el('div','cv4-calls-avatar-row');
        for (const member of active.participants.slice(0,5)) {
          const person = people.find(p => p.id === member.id);
          const name = person && person.name || 'Member';
          const initials = name.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0,2).map(s => s[0] || '').join('').toUpperCase();
          const avatar = el('span','cv4-calls-avatar',initials); avatar.title = name; avatar.setAttribute('aria-label',name); avatars.append(avatar);
        }
        avatars.append(el('span','cv4-calls-members',active.participants.length + (active.participants.length === 1 ? ' person in the call' : ' people in the call')));
        card.append(avatars);
      }
      card.append(actions); root.setAttribute('aria-busy',String(busy));
    }
    async function perform(operation) {
      if (disposed || busy) return;
      busy = true; notify(''); render();
      try { await operation(); }
      catch (error) { if (!disposed) notify(explain(error,'The call could not be completed. Please try again.')); }
      finally { busy = false; render(); }
    }
    async function refresh() {
      const sequence = ++statusSequence;
      try {
        const data = await call('calls/status?conversation_id=' + encodeURIComponent(conversationId),{signal:aborter.signal});
        if (disposed || sequence !== statusSequence) return;
        enabled = data && data.enabled === true; reason = data && data.reason || 'Calls are currently paused.';
        active = enabled ? data.active || null : null;
        if (meeting && (!enabled || !active || active.id !== pendingCallId)) await leave();
      } catch (error) {
        if (disposed || sequence !== statusSequence) return;
        enabled = false; reason = explain(error,'Call availability could not be checked.');
        if (meeting) await leave();
      }
      render();
    }
    async function start(kind) {
      if (!enabled) return;
      await perform(async () => {
        const result = await post('start',{kind,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()});
        if (disposed) return;
        active = result.call; notify('Call ready. Choose Join when you are ready to use your microphone' + (kind === 'video' ? ' and camera.' : '.'));
      });
    }
    async function join() {
      if (!enabled || !active || active.state !== 'active') return;
      await perform(async () => {
        pendingCallId = active.id;
        const result = await post('join',{call_id:pendingCallId,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()});
        ownsParticipant = true;
        let authToken = result && result.authToken;
        try {
          if (!result || !authToken || result.tokenValidityDays !== 100) throw {reason:'The call service returned an invalid participant.'};
          if (disposed || !enabled) { await revoke(); return; }
          active = result.call;
          // The only import/media boundary: explicit Join + enabled server status
          // + server-authorized participant. Start/status/mount do none of this.
          const sdk = await loadSDK();
          if (disposed || !enabled) { await revoke(); return; }
          meeting = await sdk.Client.init({authToken,audio:true,video:active.kind === 'video'});
          authToken = null; result.authToken = null;
          if (disposed || !enabled) { await stopMedia(); await revoke(); return; }
          view = el('rtk-meeting',''); view.mode = 'fill'; view.showSetupScreen = false;
          view.applyDesignSystem = false; view.leaveOnUnmount = false;
          view.addEventListener('rtkStatesUpdate',onMeetingState);
          area.replaceChildren(view); area.hidden = false; view.meeting = meeting;
          if (sdk.provideDesignSystem) sdk.provideDesignSystem(view,{theme:'light',borderRadius:'extra-rounded',
            colors:{brand:{300:'#c5e2ff',400:'#91bff0',500:'#3b7ec5',600:'#245e9d',700:'#1d4f86'},text:'#183d64','text-on-brand':'#ffffff','video-bg':'#e9f3ff'}});
          await meeting.join();
          if (!disposed) { timer = window.setInterval(() => { refresh().catch(() => {}); },15000); notify(''); }
        } catch (error) {
          await stopMedia();
          try { await revoke(); } catch { notify('The call stopped, but leaving still needs a retry.'); }
          throw error;
        } finally { authToken = null; if (result) result.authToken = null; }
      });
    }
    function onMeetingState(event) {
      if (event.detail && event.detail.meeting === 'ended') leave().catch(() => {});
    }
    async function stopMedia() {
      if (timer) { window.clearInterval(timer); timer = null; }
      const previous = meeting; meeting = null;
      if (view) { view.removeEventListener('rtkStatesUpdate',onMeetingState); view.meeting = undefined; view = null; }
      area.hidden = true; area.replaceChildren();
      if (previous) {
        try { await previous.leave(); } finally {
          // Explicitly stop local tracks even when network leave fails.
          if (previous.self) for (const track of [previous.self.audioTrack,previous.self.videoTrack,...Object.values(previous.self.screenShareTracks || {})]) if (track && typeof track.stop === 'function') track.stop();
        }
      }
    }
    async function revoke() {
      if (!ownsParticipant || !pendingCallId) return;
      await post('leave',{call_id:pendingCallId},{keepalive:disposed});
      ownsParticipant = false; pendingCallId = null;
    }
    async function leave() {
      if (leaving) return leaving;
      leaving = (async () => {
        let mediaError;
        try { await stopMedia(); } catch (error) { mediaError = error; }
        try {
          // If a reload lost local state, active.joined permits revoking the
          // same server participant before any explicit rejoin.
          if (!ownsParticipant && active && active.joined) { ownsParticipant = true; pendingCallId = active.id; }
          await revoke();
          if (active) active = {...active,joined:false};
          if (mediaError && !disposed) notify('Your media stopped. You can join again when ready.');
        } catch (error) {
          if (!disposed) notify(explain(error,'Your media stopped, but leaving still needs a retry.'));
          throw error;
        } finally { render(); }
      })();
      try { await leaving; } finally { leaving = null; }
    }
    async function end() {
      if (!enabled || !active || !active.can_end) return;
      await perform(async () => {
        const result = await post('end',{call_id:active.id});
        await stopMedia();
        if (result.ended) { ownsParticipant = false; pendingCallId = null; active = null; notify('The call has ended.'); }
        else { active = {...active,state:'ending'}; notify('The call is closing. Participant access is still being revoked; retry ending shortly.'); }
      });
    }
    async function destroy() {
      if (disposed) return;
      disposed = true; statusSequence++; aborter.abort(); window.removeEventListener('pagehide',onPageHide);
      const pending = task;
      try { await leave(); if (pending) await pending; }
      finally { if (root.parentNode === host) root.remove(); if (mounted === controller) mounted = null; }
    }
    function onPageHide() { destroy().catch(() => {}); }
    const controller = {refresh,destroy};
    // Keep all in-flight joins attached to this mount; a late result is revoked
    // instead of rendering into a different conversation after unmount.
    const originalJoin = join;
    join = function () { task = originalJoin(); return task.finally(() => { task = null; }); };
    mounted = controller; window.addEventListener('pagehide',onPageHide);
    render(); refresh().catch(() => {});
    return controller;
  }
  window.cvConnectCalls = {mount, unmount:() => mounted ? mounted.destroy() : Promise.resolve(),
    stylesHref:STYLES_URL,sdkSources:Object.freeze({core:CORE_URL,ui:UI_URL,design:DESIGN_URL})};
})();

 */

  function icon(name) {
    const svg = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'svg');
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries({viewBox:'0 0 24 24', fill:'none', stroke:'currentColor', 'stroke-width':'1.6', 'stroke-linecap':'round', 'stroke-linejoin':'round', 'aria-hidden':'true'})) svg.setAttribute(k, v);
    const p = document.createElementNS(svg.namespaceURI, 'path');
    p.setAttribute('d', paths[name] || paths.chat); svg.append(p); return svg;
  }
  function button(label, glyph, fn, cls = '') {
    const b = el('button', 'cv4-button ' + cls);
    b.type = 'button'; b.title = label; b.setAttribute('aria-label', label);
    if (glyph) b.append(icon(glyph));
    if (!cls.includes('cv4-icon')) b.append(el('span', '', label));

  }
  function orb() { const n = el('span', 'cv4-orb'); n.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); n.append(el('span')); return n; }
  function photoURL(value) {
    try {
      const raw = text(value); if (!raw.startsWith('/communiverse/') || raw.startsWith('//') || raw.includes('\\')) return '';
      const u = new URL(raw, location.origin); return u.origin === location.origin && decodeURIComponent(u.pathname).startsWith('/communiverse/') ? u.href : '';
    } catch { return ''; }
  }
  function avatar(person) {
    if (person?.id === 'cosmos' || person?.sender_id === 'cosmos') return orb();
    const name = text(person?.name || 'Member'), n = el('span', 'cv4-avatar', name.split(/\s+/).slice(0,2).map(x=>x[0]||'').join('').toUpperCase());
    n.title = name; n.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    const url = photoURL(person?.photo || person?.photo_url);
    if (url) { const img = el('img'); img.src=url; img.alt=''; img.width=36; img.height=36; img.loading='lazy'; img.addEventListener('error',()=>img.remove(),{once:true}); n.append(img); }
    return n;
  }
  function fileURL(file) {
    if (!file?.id) return '';
    // Never trust an API/model-supplied attachment URL, including external URLs.
    return PREFIX + 'files/file?id=' + encodeURIComponent(text(file.id));
  }
  function recordURL(value) {
    try {
      const raw = text(value);
      if (!raw.startsWith('/communiverse/') || /[\\\u0000-\u0020\u007f]/.test(raw)) return '';
      const u = new URL(raw, location.origin),path=decodeURIComponent(u.pathname);
      if (u.origin !== location.origin || !path.startsWith('/communiverse/') || /\/api(?:\/|$)/i.test(path) || /[\\%\u0000-\u0020\u007f]/.test(path)) return '';
      return u.pathname + u.search + u.hash;
    } catch { return ''; }
  }
  // Only this browser's local draft metadata is cleared; this never grants,
  // revokes or writes document permissions. Bodies/files retain their old flow.
  function clearToolDrafts(actorId, conversationId = '') {
    const prefix=text(actorId)+':'+(conversationId?conversationId+':':'');
    const clear=d=>({...d,documents:[],requestId:'',fingerprint:''});
    for(const [key,d]of memoryDrafts)if(key.startsWith(prefix))memoryDrafts.set(key,clear(d));
    try{
      for(let i=sessionStorage.length-1;i>=0;i--){
        const key=sessionStorage.key(i);if(!key?.startsWith('cv4-draft:'+prefix))continue;
        const d=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(key)||'null');if(d)sessionStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify(clear(d)));
      }
    }catch{}
  }
  function date(value) { const v=text(value); const d=new Date(v.includes('T')?v:v.replace(' ','T')+'Z'); return Number.isNaN(d.getTime())?null:d; }
  function time(value) { const d=date(value); return d?new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{hour:'numeric',minute:'2-digit'}).format(d):''; }
  function day(value) { const d=date(value); return d?new Intl.DateTimeFormat(undefined,{month:'short',day:'numeric',year:'numeric'}).format(d):''; }
  function bytes(value) { const n=Number(value)||0; return n>1048576?(n/1048576).toFixed(1)+' MB':Math.max(1,Math.round(n/1024))+' KB'; }
  function mediaType(value) {
    const type=text(value).split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase();
    return ({'audio/m4a':'audio/mp4','audio/x-m4a':'audio/mp4','audio/x-wav':'audio/wav','audio/wave':'audio/wav','audio/x-webm':'audio/webm','audio/x-ogg':'audio/ogg'})[type]||type;
  }
  function audioTime(value) {
    const seconds=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(value)||0)),hours=Math.floor(seconds/3600);
    return (hours?hours+':':'')+(hours?String(Math.floor(seconds/60)%60).padStart(2,'0'):Math.floor(seconds/60))+':'+String(seconds%60).padStart(2,'0');
  }
  function voiceFile(file) {
    return file.voice===true||/^(?:voice(?:[ _-]*(?:note|message))?|recording)(?:[ _-].*)?\.(?:webm|m4a|mp4|ogg|wav)$/i.test(text(file.name));
  }
  function messagePreview(message) {
    const body=message&&typeof message==='object'?message.body:message;
    if(typeof body==='string'&&body.trim())return body;
    if(message?.structured?.source_unavailable||message?.structured?.title==='Source unavailable')return 'Source unavailable';
    if(message?.structured?.kind==='tool_reference')return 'Tools reference';
    return list(message?.attachments).some(voiceFile)?'Voice message':'Attachment';
  }

    function connection(value,state) { if(!alive)return;status.textContent=value;status.dataset.state=state; }
    function pauseMedia(target) { target.querySelectorAll('audio,video').forEach(media=>media.pause()); }
    function announce(value) { if(alive)announcements.textContent=value; }
    function notify(value, retry) {
      if(!alive)return;notice.replaceChildren(el('span','',value));notice.hidden=!value;

    }
    function stateFor(id) {
      if(!rooms.has(id))rooms.set(id,{messages:[],nextCursor:null,readSequence:0,ack:0,loaded:false,loading:false,error:'',participants:[],request:0});
      return rooms.get(id);
    }
    function conversation(id=active) { return conversations.find(c=>text(c.id)===text(id)); }
    function personFor(id, participants=[]) { return text(id)==='cosmos'?{id:'cosmos',name:'Cosmos'}:participants.find(p=>text(p.id)===text(id))||people.find(p=>text(p.id)===text(id))||(person?.id===id?person:{id,name:'Member'}); }
    function roomTitle(c) { if(c?.kind==='dm'){const p=list(c.participants).find(p=>p.id!==person?.id);return text(p?.name||c.title||'Direct message');}return text(c?.title||'Conversation'); }
    function updateConversation(value) {
      if(!value?.id)return;const at=conversations.findIndex(c=>c.id===value.id);
      if(at<0)conversations.push(value);else conversations[at]={...conversations[at],...value};
    }
    function requestError(data,statusCode) {
      const detail=typeof data?.error==='string'?data.error:typeof data?.error?.message==='string'?data.error.message:text(data?.message);
      const conflict=statusCode===409&&(!data?.code||data.code==='version_conflict');
      const error=new Error(statusCode===401?'Your session expired. Sign in again to open Connect.':conflict?'This message changed elsewhere. Review the latest version and try again.':detail||('Could not complete the request ('+statusCode+'). Try again.'));
      error.status=statusCode;error.code=data?.code||data?.error?.code;return error;
    }
    async function api(path, options = {}) {
      if(!alive)throw new DOMException('Connect has closed.','AbortError');
      if(sessionLocked)throw requestError({},401);
      if(!/^[a-z][a-z0-9/-]*(\?.*)?$/.test(path)||path.split('?')[0].includes('..'))throw new Error('Invalid Connect API path.');
      const abort=new AbortController();pending.add(abort);
      const externalAbort=()=>abort.abort();options.signal?.addEventListener('abort',externalAbort,{once:true});if(options.signal?.aborted)abort.abort();
      const timeout=delay(()=>abort.abort(),path==='cosmos'?40000:20000);
      const headers={Accept:'application/json',...options.headers}, init={...options,headers,credentials:'same-origin',cache:'no-store',signal:abort.signal};
      if(options.json!==undefined){init.body=JSON.stringify(options.json);headers['Content-Type']='application/json';delete init.json;}
      try{
        const response=await fetch(PREFIX+path,init);
        let data;try{data=await response.json();}catch{throw new Error('Connect received an unreadable response. Try again.');}
        if(!response.ok){if(response.status===401)lockSession();throw requestError(data,response.status);}
        if(!alive||sessionLocked)throw new DOMException('Connect has closed.','AbortError');
        return data;
      }catch(error){if(error.name==='AbortError'&&alive)throw new Error('The request timed out. Your draft is saved; try again.');throw error;}
      finally{cancel(timeout);pending.delete(abort);options.signal?.removeEventListener('abort',externalAbort);}
    }
    const write=(path,json)=>api(path,{method:'POST',json});
    function lockSession() {
      if(sessionLocked)return;sessionLocked=true;pauseMedia(root);closeSocket();cancel(pollTimer);cancel(reconnectTimer);stopRecording(true);if(aux?.kind==='calls')window.cvConnectCalls?.unmount?.();
      for(const ctx of composers){closeToolPicker(ctx);ctx.documents=[];if(!ctx.isCosmos)discardVoice(ctx,false,true);}
      clearToolDrafts(person?.id);
      rooms.clear();threads.clear();conversations=[];people=[];composers.clear();aux=null;auxiliary.replaceChildren();auxiliary.hidden=true;
      timeline.replaceChildren();composerHost.replaceChildren();header.replaceChildren();
      main.hidden=false;setPane('conversation');timeline.append(empty('Sign in to continue','Your session has expired. Open Connect again after signing in.'));
      sidebarList.replaceChildren();connection('Session expired','error');notify('Your session expired. Sign in again to open Connect.');
    }
    function invalidateRoom(id,error) {
      for(const ctx of composers)if(ctx.id===id){closeToolPicker(ctx);ctx.documents=[];}
      clearToolDrafts(person?.id,id);
      rooms.delete(id);for(const [key,value]of threads)if(value.conversationId===id)threads.delete(key);
      conversations=conversations.filter(c=>c.id!==id);
      if(active===id){
        pauseMedia(root);active='';closeSocket();cancel(pollTimer);stopRecording(true);closeAux();composerHost.replaceChildren();
        if(section==='messages'){header.replaceChildren();timeline.replaceChildren(empty('Conversation unavailable','Your membership could not be verified. Reopen Connect to check your access.'));}
      }
      for(const ctx of composers)if(ctx.id===id&&!ctx.isCosmos)discardVoice(ctx,false,true);
      paintSidebar();notify(error.message);
    }
    function empty(title,body,action) { const n=el('div','cv4-empty');n.append(el('h3','',title),el('p','',body));if(action)n.append(action);return n; }
    function skeleton(target,label) {
      target.replaceChildren();target.setAttribute('aria-busy','true');
      const loading=el('div','cv4-loading');loading.setAttribute('role','status');loading.append(el('p','',label));
      for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const row=el('div','cv4-skeleton');row.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');row.append(el('span'),el('span'));loading.append(row);}
      target.append(loading);
    }
    function setPane(value) { pane=value;root.dataset.pane=value;main.hidden=false;queueRead(); }
    function paintTabs() { messagesTab.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(section==='messages'));peopleTab.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(section==='people')); }
    function paintSidebar() {
      if(!alive)return;paintTabs();sidebarList.replaceChildren();sidebarList.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
      const filtered=conversations.filter(c=>(!unreadOnly||Number(c.unread)>0)&&(!sidebarQuery||roomTitle(c).toLocaleLowerCase().includes(sidebarQuery)));
      const groups=[['Starred',filtered.filter(c=>c.starred)],['Channels',filtered.filter(c=>!c.starred&&c.kind==='channel')],['Direct messages',filtered.filter(c=>!c.starred&&c.kind==='dm')],['Groups',filtered.filter(c=>!c.starred&&c.kind==='group')]];
      for(const [label,items]of groups){
        if(!items.length)continue;const group=el('section','cv4-sidebar-group');group.append(el('h3','cv4-kicker',label));
        for(const c of items){
          const b=button(roomTitle(c),null,()=>openConversation(c.id),'cv4-room');
          b.replaceChildren();b.dataset.conversation=c.id;b.setAttribute('aria-current',String(active===c.id&&section==='messages'));b.title=roomTitle(c);
          if(c.kind==='dm')b.append(avatar(list(c.participants).find(p=>p.id!==person?.id)));else b.append(icon(c.private?'lock':'hash'));
          const copy=el('span','cv4-room-copy');copy.append(el('span','cv4-room-title',roomTitle(c)));
          if(c.last_message)copy.append(el('small','',messagePreview(c.last_message)));
          b.append(copy);
          if(Number(c.unread)>0){const badge=el('span','cv4-badge',Number(c.unread)>99?'99+':c.unread);badge.setAttribute('aria-label',c.unread+' unread messages');b.append(badge);}
          if(c.muted){const muted=el('span','cv4-sr','Muted');b.append(muted);}group.append(b);
        }sidebarList.append(group);
      }
      if(!filtered.length)sidebarList.append(empty(unreadOnly?'You’re all caught up':'No conversations yet',sidebarQuery?'Try another name.':'Start a direct message or create a channel.'));
      if(available.length&&!sidebarQuery&&!unreadOnly){
        const group=el('section','cv4-sidebar-group');group.append(el('h3','cv4-kicker','Discover channels'));
        for(const c of available.filter(c=>!conversation(c.id))){const row=el('div','cv4-discover');row.append(el('span','',roomTitle(c)),button('Join','plus',async()=>{
          try{await write('members',{conversation_id:c.id,action:'join',identities:[]});await refreshConversations();await openConversation(c.id);}catch(error){notify(error.message);}
        }));group.append(row);}sidebarList.append(group);
      }
      const unreads=conversations.reduce((n,c)=>n+(Number(c.unread)||0),0);unreadButton.title=unreads+' unread messages';unreadButton.setAttribute('aria-label','Unread · '+unreads+' unread messages');
    }
    async function refreshConversations() {
      const d=await api('conversations');if(!alive)return;
      const next=Array.isArray(d)?d:list(d.conversations);conversations=next;available=list(d.available_channels);paintSidebar();
      if(active&&!conversation())invalidateRoom(active,new Error('This conversation is no longer available to your account.'));
    }
    function showSection(value) {
      if(!alive||sessionLocked)return;
      // Remember navigation before bootstrap; only bootstrap supplies identity.
      if(!person){
        section=value;paintTabs();setPane(value==='people'?'people':'sidebar');
        if(value==='people'){
          main.setAttribute('aria-label','People directory');header.replaceChildren(button('Back to messages','back',()=>showSection('messages'),'cv4-icon cv4-mobile-back'),el('h3','','People'));
          skeleton(timeline,'Loading people…');
        }else{header.replaceChildren();skeleton(timeline,'Opening Connect…');}
        return;
      }
      sendTyping(false);stopRecording(true);saveCurrentDrafts();
      for(const ctx of composers)closeToolPicker(ctx);
      if(value==='people')for(const ctx of composers)releaseVoicePreview(ctx);
      section=value;closeAux();paintTabs();paintSidebar();
      if(value==='people'){setPane('people');paintPeople();closeSocket();connection('People ready','ready');}
      else if(active){setPane('conversation');renderRoom();connect();}
      else{setPane('sidebar');paintWelcome();}
    }
    function backToSidebar() { stopRecording(true);closeAux();for(const ctx of composers){closeToolPicker(ctx);releaseVoicePreview(ctx);}setPane('sidebar');sendTyping(false);queueRead(); }
    function paintWelcome() {
      header.replaceChildren(el('h3','','Your conversations'));composerHost.replaceChildren();
      pauseMedia(timeline);timeline.replaceChildren(empty('Start a conversation','Choose a channel or open People to start a direct message.',button('Browse people','people',()=>showSection('people'))));timeline.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
    }
    function paintPeople() {
      pauseMedia(main);main.setAttribute('aria-label','People directory');header.replaceChildren();
      const back=button('Back to messages','back',()=>showSection('messages'),'cv4-icon cv4-mobile-back');
      header.append(back,el('h3','','People'));composerHost.replaceChildren();timeline.replaceChildren();timeline.removeAttribute('aria-busy');timeline.setAttribute('aria-label','People directory');
      const tools=el('div','cv4-people-tools'), label=el('label','','Search people'), input=el('input','cv4-input');
      input.type='search';input.placeholder='Search name or role';input.value=peopleQuery;input.id=instanceId+'-people';label.htmlFor=input.id;
      const grid=el('div','cv4-people-grid');
      const paint=()=>{
        grid.replaceChildren();const q=peopleQuery.toLocaleLowerCase();
        for(const p of people.filter(p=>p.id!==person.id&&p.id!=='cosmos'&&!p.bot&&(!q||(text(p.name)+' '+text(p.role)).toLocaleLowerCase().includes(q)))){
          const card=el('article','cv4-person-card'),copy=el('div','cv4-person-copy');
          copy.append(el('h4','',p.name||'Team member'),el('p','',p.role||'Team member'));
          const start=button('Message','chat',()=>startDM(p,start));start.dataset.person=p.id;card.append(avatar(p),copy,start);grid.append(card);
        }
        if(!grid.children.length)grid.append(empty('No matching people','Try a different name or role.'));
      };
      input.addEventListener('input',()=>{peopleQuery=input.value.trim();paint();});tools.append(label,input);timeline.append(tools,grid);paint();
    }
    async function startDM(p,b) {
      b.disabled=true;const existing=conversations.find(c=>c.kind==='dm'&&list(c.participants).some(x=>x.id===p.id));
      try{if(existing)await openConversation(existing.id);else{
        const d=await write('conversations',{kind:'dm',title:'',participants:[p.id],private:true,topic:'',requestId:uid()});
        const c=d.conversation||d;updateConversation(c);
        if(!c.id)throw new Error('Conversation could not be opened. Try again.');await openConversation(c.id);
      }}catch(error){notify(error.message,()=>startDM(p,b));}finally{b.disabled=false;}
    }
    async function openConversation(id) {
      if(sessionLocked||!alive)return;id=text(id);if(!id)return;
      saveCurrentDrafts();sendTyping(false);pauseMedia(root);stopRecording(true);closeSocket();closeAux();
      for(const ctx of composers){closeToolPicker(ctx);releaseVoicePreview(ctx);}composers.clear();
      active=id;section='messages';setPane('conversation');main.setAttribute('aria-label','Conversation');
      timeline.setAttribute('aria-label','Conversation messages');paintSidebar();paintHeader();composerHost.replaceChildren();
      if(stateFor(id).loaded)renderRoom();else skeleton(timeline,'Loading conversation…');
      try{await loadMessages(id);if(alive&&active===id){renderRoom();connect();}}
      catch(error){if(!alive||sessionLocked)return;if(error.status===403||error.status===404)invalidateRoom(id,error);else if(active===id&&section==='messages'){
        timeline.removeAttribute('aria-busy');timeline.replaceChildren(empty('Messages could not load',error.message,button('Try again','refresh',()=>openConversation(id))));connection('Messages unavailable','error');
      }}
    }
    async function loadMessages(id,{before,root:rootId}={}) {
      const key=id+':'+(rootId||''), st=rootId?(threads.get(key)||{messages:[],nextCursor:null,loaded:false,conversationId:id,request:0}):stateFor(id);
      if(rootId)threads.set(key,st);
      const token=++st.request;
      const params=new URLSearchParams({conversation_id:id});
      if(before!==undefined&&before!==null)params.set('before',text(before));
      if(rootId)params.set('root',rootId);
      const data=await api('messages?'+params);if(!alive||token!==st.request)return st;
      updateConversation(data.conversation);st.participants=list(data.participants).length?data.participants:list(data.conversation?.participants);
      const fetched=list(data.messages).filter(m=>m.id&&text(m.conversation_id||m.thread_id||id)===id);
      const merged=new Map(st.messages.map(m=>[m.id,m]));
      for(const message of fetched)merged.set(message.id,message);
      st.messages=[...merged.values()].sort((a,b)=>Number(a.sequence)-Number(b.sequence));
      // Poll refresh never erases an older-page cursor.
      if(before!==undefined||!st.loaded)st.nextCursor=data.nextCursor??null;
      st.loaded=true;st.readSequence=Math.max(st.readSequence||0,Number(data.readSequence)||0);st.ack=Math.max(st.ack||0,st.readSequence);
      st.error='';return st;
    }
    function paintHeader() {
      header.replaceChildren();const c=conversation();if(!c){header.append(el('h3','','Conversation'));return;}
      const back=button('All conversations','back',backToSidebar,'cv4-icon cv4-mobile-back');header.append(back);
      if(c.kind==='dm')header.append(avatar(list(c.participants).find(p=>p.id!==person.id)));else header.append(icon(c.private?'lock':'hash'));
      const copy=el('div','cv4-header-copy');copy.append(el('h3','',roomTitle(c)),el('p','',c.topic||(c.kind==='dm'?'Direct message':c.private?'Private channel':'Shared channel')));
      const actions=el('div','cv4-header-actions'), star=button(c.starred?'Unstar conversation':'Star conversation','star',()=>preference('starred',!c.starred),'cv4-icon');
      star.dataset.action='star';star.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!c.starred));
      const members=button('Members','people',()=>showMembers(),'cv4-icon');members.dataset.action='members';
      const more=el('details','cv4-more'), summary=el('summary','cv4-button cv4-icon');summary.title='Conversation actions';summary.setAttribute('aria-label','Conversation actions');summary.append(icon('more'));
      const menu=el('div','cv4-more-menu'), mute=button(c.muted?'Unmute conversation':'Mute conversation','bell',()=>{more.open=false;preference('muted',!c.muted);});mute.dataset.action='mute';
      const call=button('Voice and video','video',()=>{more.open=false;showCalls();});call.dataset.action='calls';
      menu.append(mute,call,button('Find in conversation','search',()=>{more.open=false;showSearch('',true);}));
      more.append(summary,menu);actions.append(star,members,more);
      const ask=button('Ask Cosmos',null,()=>showCosmos());ask.prepend(orb());ask.dataset.action='cosmos';actions.append(ask);
      header.append(copy,actions);
    }
    async function preference(name,value) {
      const id=active,c=conversation(id);if(!c)return;
      try{await write('preferences',{conversation_id:id,[name]:value});if(alive&&conversation(id)){c[name]=value;paintSidebar();if(active===id)paintHeader();}}
      catch(error){if(error.status===403)invalidateRoom(id,error);else notify(error.message);}
    }
    function renderRoom() {
      if(!alive||!active||section!=='messages')return;paintHeader();
      const st=stateFor(active);paintMessages(timeline,st,false);
      if(!composerHost.querySelector('form')||composerHost.dataset.conversation!==active){
        for(const ctx of composers)if(!ctx.rootId)composers.delete(ctx);
        composerHost.replaceChildren();composerHost.dataset.conversation=active;composerHost.append(createComposer(active,'').form);
      }
      paintSidebar();queueRead();
    }
    function paintMessages(target,st,isThread) {
      const bottom=target.scrollHeight-target.scrollTop-target.clientHeight<100, oldHeight=target.scrollHeight, oldTop=target.scrollTop;
      const audioCards=new Map([...target.querySelectorAll('.cv4-audio-card[data-audio-file]')].map(card=>[card.dataset.audioFile,card]));
      const focusedAudio=document.activeElement?.closest?.('.cv4-audio-card')&&target.contains(document.activeElement)?document.activeElement:null;
      readObserver?.disconnect();target.replaceChildren();target.removeAttribute('aria-busy');
      if(st.nextCursor!=null){
        const older=button('Load earlier messages','refresh',async()=>{
          const before=st.nextCursor, id=active, rootId=isThread?aux?.rootId:'', height=target.scrollHeight, top=target.scrollTop;older.disabled=true;
          try{await loadMessages(id,{before,root:rootId});if(!alive||active!==id||isThread&&aux?.rootId!==rootId)return;
            paintMessages(target,st,isThread);target.scrollTop=top+target.scrollHeight-height;queueRead();
          }catch(error){if(error.status===403)invalidateRoom(id,error);else{older.disabled=false;notify(error.message);}}
        });older.dataset.action=isThread?'older-replies':'older';target.append(older);
      }
      let previousDay='';
      for(const m of st.messages){
        const nextDay=day(m.created_at);
        if(nextDay&&nextDay!==previousDay){const d=el('p','cv4-date',nextDay);target.append(d);previousDay=nextDay;}
        if(!isThread&&!m.reply_to&&Number(m.sequence)>st.readSequence&&st.readSequence>0&&st.messages.filter(x=>Number(x.sequence)>st.readSequence&&!x.reply_to)[0]?.id===m.id){target.append(el('p','cv4-unread-divider','New messages'));}
        target.append(messageNode(m,st.participants,isThread,audioCards));
      }
      for(const card of audioCards.values())pauseMedia(card);
      if(!st.messages.length)target.append(empty(isThread?'No replies yet':'No messages yet',isThread?'Reply here to keep this discussion in one thread.':'Write a message, attach a file or record a voice message.'));
      if(bottom||!oldHeight)target.scrollTop=target.scrollHeight;else target.scrollTop=oldTop;
      if(focusedAudio?.isConnected&&target.contains(focusedAudio))focusedAudio.focus({preventScroll:true});
      observeReads();queueRead();
    }
    function messageNode(m,participants,isThread=false,audioCards) {
      const who=personFor(m.sender_id,participants), own=m.sender_id===person?.id;
      const article=el('article','cv4-message'+(own?' cv4-own':'')+(m.sender_id==='cosmos'?' cv4-cosmos-message':''));
      article.dataset.message=m.id;article.dataset.sequence=text(m.sequence);article.dataset.conversation=active;article.tabIndex=-1;
      article.append(avatar(who));const copy=el('div','cv4-message-copy'),meta=el('div','cv4-message-meta'),t=el('time','',time(m.created_at));
      if(date(m.created_at))t.dateTime=date(m.created_at).toISOString();t.title=day(m.created_at)+' '+time(m.created_at);meta.append(el('span','cv4-sender',who.name),t);
      if(m.edited_at&&!m.deleted_at)meta.append(el('small','','Edited'));copy.append(meta);
      if(m.deleted_at){copy.append(el('p','cv4-tombstone','This message was deleted.'));article.append(copy);return article;}
      if(m.reply_to&&!isThread){
        const parent=stateFor(active).messages.find(x=>x.id===m.reply_to),b=button('Reply in thread','reply',()=>openThread(m.reply_to),'cv4-thread-context');
        b.append(el('span','',parent?.deleted_at?'Deleted message':text(parent?.body||'Open original message').slice(0,90)));copy.append(b);
      }
      if(m.body&&!(m.sender_id==='cosmos'&&(m.structured?.source_unavailable||m.structured?.title==='Source unavailable')))copy.append(el('p','cv4-message-body',m.body));
      if(m.structured&&(m.sender_id==='cosmos'||m.structured.kind==='tool_reference'||m.structured.source_unavailable||m.structured.title==='Source unavailable'))copy.append(structuredCard(m.structured));
      if(list(m.attachments).length){const attachments=el('div','cv4-attachments');for(const f of m.attachments){const reuse=audioCards?.get(text(f.id));audioCards?.delete(text(f.id));attachments.append(attachment(f,undefined,reuse));}copy.append(attachments);}
      const reactions=el('div','cv4-reactions');
      for(const r of list(m.reactions)){
        const b=button(text(r.emoji)+' '+(Number(r.count)||0),null,()=>react(m,r.emoji));
        b.classList.add('cv4-reaction');b.dataset.emoji=r.emoji;b.setAttribute('aria-label',r.emoji+' · '+r.count+' reactions'+(r.me?' · you reacted':''));b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!r.me));reactions.append(b);
      }copy.append(reactions);
      const actions=el('div','cv4-message-actions');
      const reply=button(isThread?'Reply to thread':'Reply in thread','reply',()=>{if(isThread){auxiliary.querySelector('textarea')?.focus();}else openThread(m.reply_to||m.id);});
      reply.dataset.action='reply';actions.append(reply);
      const picker=el('details','cv4-reaction-picker'),summary=el('summary','cv4-button cv4-icon');summary.setAttribute('aria-label','Add a reaction');summary.title='Add a reaction';summary.append(icon('smile'));
      const choices=el('div','cv4-reaction-choices');
      for(const emoji of ['👍','❤️','🎉','✅','👀','🙏']){const b=button(emoji,null,()=>{picker.open=false;react(m,emoji);});b.setAttribute('aria-label','React '+emoji);choices.append(b);}
      picker.append(summary,choices);actions.append(picker);
      if(own){
        const edit=button('Edit','edit',()=>showEdit(m));edit.dataset.action='edit';
        const del=button('Delete','trash',()=>showDelete(m));del.dataset.action='delete';actions.append(edit,del);
      }copy.append(actions);article.append(copy);return article;
    }
    function audioCard(file,url,local,reuse) {
      const voice=voiceFile(file),title=voice?'Voice message':'Audio file',name=text(file.name||'Audio file'),type=mediaType(file.content_type||file.type).replace(/^video\/(mp4|webm)$/,'audio/$1');
      const signature=JSON.stringify([url,name,type]);
      if(reuse?.dataset.audioSignature===signature)return reuse;
      if(reuse)pauseMedia(reuse);
      const box=el('figure','cv4-file-card cv4-audio-card');box.dataset.audioFile=text(file.id||file.localId||url);box.dataset.audioSignature=signature;
      const id=instanceId+'-audio-'+(++serial);box.setAttribute('role','group');box.setAttribute('aria-labelledby',id+'-title');
      const audio=el('audio','cv4-audio-source');audio.preload='metadata';audio.controls=false;audio.setAttribute('aria-label',title);audio.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      const caption=el('figcaption'),top=el('div','cv4-audio-top'),heading=el('span','cv4-audio-title',title),clock=el('span','cv4-audio-time','0:00');
      heading.id=id+'-title';clock.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
      const statusNode=el('p','cv4-audio-status','Loading audio…');statusNode.id=id+'-status';statusNode.setAttribute('role','status');statusNode.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');
      const durationNote=el('small','cv4-audio-duration-note','Duration unavailable');durationNote.hidden=true;
      const seekLabel=el('label','cv4-sr','Seek '+title.toLowerCase()),seek=el('input','cv4-audio-seek');seek.type='range';seek.min='0';seek.max='0';seek.step='0.1';seek.value='0';seek.disabled=true;
      seek.id=id+'-seek';seekLabel.htmlFor=seek.id;seek.setAttribute('aria-describedby',statusNode.id);
      let requested=false,failed=false,seekRanges=[];
      const play=button('Play '+title.toLowerCase(),'play',async()=>{
        if(!audio.paused||requested){requested=false;audio.pause();render();return;}
        if(failed){failed=false;audio.load();}requested=true;statusNode.textContent='Loading audio…';render();
        try{await audio.play();if(!alive||!audio.isConnected||!requested)audio.pause();}
        catch(error){requested=false;if(error.name==='AbortError'){render();return;}if(error.name==='NotAllowedError'){statusNode.textContent='Select Play to start the audio.';render();}else showError();}
      },'cv4-icon cv4-audio-play');
      play.setAttribute('aria-describedby',statusNode.id);
      function duration(){const value=Number(audio.duration);return Number.isFinite(value)&&value>0?value:null;}
      function render(){
        const length=duration(),position=Number.isFinite(audio.currentTime)?Math.max(0,audio.currentTime):0;
        seekRanges=[];
        try{for(let i=0;i<audio.seekable.length;i++){const start=audio.seekable.start(i),end=audio.seekable.end(i);if(Number.isFinite(start)&&Number.isFinite(end)&&end>start)seekRanges.push([start,end]);}}catch{}
        const canSeek=!!length&&seekRanges.length>0&&!failed;
        seek.disabled=!canSeek;seek.min=canSeek?String(seekRanges[0][0]):'0';seek.max=canSeek?String(Math.min(length,seekRanges.at(-1)[1])):'0';
        seek.value=String(canSeek?Math.min(Number(seek.max),Math.max(Number(seek.min),position)):0);
        seek.setAttribute('aria-valuetext',audioTime(position)+(length?' of '+audioTime(length):' · duration unavailable'));
        seek.title=canSeek?'Seek '+title.toLowerCase():'Seeking is unavailable until the audio duration is known.';
        clock.textContent=audioTime(position)+(length?' / '+audioTime(length):'');
        clock.title=length?'Elapsed time / duration':'Elapsed time · duration unavailable';
        durationNote.hidden=!!length||audio.readyState<1||failed;
        durationNote.textContent=voice&&Number(file.recorded_ms)>0?'Recorded '+audioTime(file.recorded_ms/1000)+' · seeking unavailable':'Duration unavailable';
        const playing=!audio.paused||requested,label=(failed?'Retry':playing?'Pause':'Play')+' '+title.toLowerCase();
        play.setAttribute('aria-label',label);play.title=label;play.replaceChildren(icon(playing?'pause':'play'));
        box.dataset.audioState=failed?'error':playing?'playing':audio.readyState>=1?'ready':'loading';
      }
      function showError(){requested=false;failed=true;audio.pause();statusNode.textContent=local?'Preview unavailable. Download the audio to listen.':'Audio could not load. Retry or download it to listen.';render();}
      audio.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',()=>{failed=false;statusNode.textContent='';render();});
      audio.addEventListener('durationchange',render);audio.addEventListener('progress',render);audio.addEventListener('timeupdate',render);
      audio.addEventListener('canplay',()=>{if(!failed)statusNode.textContent='';render();});
      audio.addEventListener('play',()=>{requested=true;for(const other of root.querySelectorAll('.cv4-audio-source'))if(other!==audio)other.pause();render();});
      audio.addEventListener('playing',()=>{statusNode.textContent='';render();});
      audio.addEventListener('pause',()=>{requested=false;if(!failed)statusNode.textContent='';render();});
      audio.addEventListener('ended',()=>{requested=false;statusNode.textContent='';render();});
      audio.addEventListener('waiting',()=>{if(requested)statusNode.textContent='Loading audio…';});
      audio.addEventListener('stalled',()=>{if(requested)statusNode.textContent='Audio is still loading. You can pause or download it.';});
      audio.addEventListener('error',showError);
      seek.addEventListener('input',()=>{
        const value=Number(seek.value);if(seek.disabled||!Number.isFinite(value))return;
        if(!seekRanges.some(([start,end])=>value>=start&&value<=end)){statusNode.textContent='That part of the audio is still loading.';render();return;}
        try{audio.currentTime=value;render();}catch{statusNode.textContent='Seeking is unavailable. You can download the audio.';}
      });
      top.append(play,heading,clock);caption.append(top);
      if(!voice){const filename=el('span','cv4-audio-filename',name);filename.title=name;caption.append(filename);}
      const footer=el('div','cv4-audio-footer'),size=el('small','',bytes(file.bytes||file.size)),download=el('a','cv4-button cv4-download','Download audio');
      download.href=url;download.download=voice?'Voice message.'+({ 'audio/mp4':'m4a','audio/webm':'webm','audio/ogg':'ogg','audio/wav':'wav','audio/mpeg':'mp3'}[type]||'audio'):name;
      download.setAttribute('aria-label',voice?'Download voice message':'Download '+name);
      if(voice){
        // Voice bubbles are not file cards. Reveal the private/local download
        // escape route only if playback actually fails; ordinary audio keeps it.
        box.classList.add('cv4-voice-bubble');footer.hidden=true;footer.append(download);
        audio.addEventListener('error',()=>footer.hidden=false);
        audio.addEventListener('canplay',()=>footer.hidden=true);
        if(local&&file.voice===true)audio.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',()=>{if(Number.isFinite(audio.duration)&&audio.duration>0)file.duration_ms=Math.round(audio.duration*1000);});
      }else footer.append(size,download);
      caption.append(seekLabel,seek,durationNote,statusNode,footer);box.append(audio,caption);if(url)audio.src=url;else showError();render();return box;
    }
    function attachment(file,localURL,reuse) {
      const local=localURL&&objectURLs.has(localURL)&&localURL.startsWith('blob:')?localURL:'';
      const url=local||fileURL(file),rawType=mediaType(file.content_type||file.type),name=text(file.name||'Attachment');
      // Older audio-only recorder uploads can carry a video container MIME.
      // Use an audio player only for recorded voice filenames/metadata.
      const type=voiceFile(file)&&/^video\/(mp4|webm)$/.test(rawType)?rawType.replace('video/','audio/'):rawType;
      if(/^audio\/(webm|ogg|mp4|mpeg|wav|aac)$/.test(type)&&url)return audioCard(file,url,!!local,reuse);
      if(reuse)pauseMedia(reuse);
      const box=el('figure','cv4-file-card');
      let media=null;
      if(/^image\/(jpeg|png|webp)$/.test(type)){media=el('img');media.alt=name;media.loading='lazy';media.width=280;media.height=180;}
      else if(/^video\/(mp4|webm|quicktime)$/.test(type)){media=el('video');media.controls=true;media.playsInline=true;media.preload='metadata';media.setAttribute('aria-label',name);}
      if(media&&url){media.src=url;media.addEventListener('error',()=>{const fallback=el('p','cv4-media-error','Preview unavailable. Download the attachment to open it.');if(!box.querySelector('.cv4-media-error'))box.append(fallback);},{once:true});box.append(media);}
      const caption=el('figcaption');caption.append(el('span','',name),el('small','',bytes(file.bytes||file.size)));
      if(!local&&url){const a=el('a','cv4-button cv4-download','Download');a.href=url;a.download=name;caption.append(a);}
      box.append(caption);return box;
    }
    function structuredCard(answer) {
      const reference=answer.kind==='tool_reference',unavailable=answer.source_unavailable===true||answer.title==='Source unavailable';
      const card=el('section','cv4-structured'+(reference?' cv4-tool-reference':''));card.setAttribute('aria-label',unavailable?'Source unavailable':reference?'Tools references':'Cosmos answer');
      card.append(el('h4','',unavailable?'Source unavailable':text(answer.title||(reference?'Tools references':'Cosmos'))));
      if(answer.summary)card.append(el('p','',answer.summary));
      if(unavailable){card.dataset.sourceState='unavailable';return card;}
      if(reference)card.append(el('small','cv4-tool-scope','Only shared with this conversation'));
      for(const s of list(answer.sections).slice(0,12)){
        const d=el('details','cv4-answer-section'),summary=el('summary','',text(s.title||'Details'));d.open=true;d.append(summary);
        if(s.body)d.append(el('p','',s.body));if(list(s.items).length){const ul=el('ul');for(const item of s.items.slice(0,30))ul.append(el('li','',typeof item==='string'?item:item?.text||item?.title||''));d.append(ul);}card.append(d);
      }
      const records=el('div','cv4-answer-records');
      for(const r of list(answer.records).filter(r=>r&&typeof r==='object').slice(0,20)){
        if(reference||r.kind==='documents'){
          records.append(toolSourceCard(r,list(answer.tool_sources).find(s=>s.id===(r.document_id||r.id))?.version));continue;
        }
        const item=el('article','cv4-answer-record');item.append(el('small','',r.kind||'Record'),el('h5','',r.title||'Details'));
        if(r.status||r.owner_name||r.due_date)item.append(el('p','',[r.status,r.owner_name&&'Owner: '+r.owner_name,r.due_date&&'Due: '+r.due_date].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')));
        const path=recordURL(r.url);
        if(path){const a=el('a','cv4-button','Open details');a.href=path;item.append(a);}records.append(item);
      }card.append(records);
      if(reference)return card;
      const actions=el('div','cv4-answer-actions');
      for(const s of list(answer.suggestions).filter(s=>typeof s==='string').slice(0,6))actions.append(button(s,null,()=>showCosmos(s)));
      actions.append(button('Continue privately',null,()=>invokePrivate(text(answer.summary||answer.title||''))));
      card.append(actions);return card;
    }
    function replaceMessage(m) {
      if(!m?.id)return;
      for(const st of [...rooms.values(),...threads.values()]){const at=st.messages.findIndex(x=>x.id===m.id);if(at>=0)st.messages[at]={...st.messages[at],...m};}
      if(section==='messages')paintMessages(timeline,stateFor(active),false);
      if(aux?.kind==='thread'){const st=threads.get(active+':'+aux.rootId),log=auxiliary.querySelector('.cv4-thread-log');if(st&&log)paintThread(st,log);}
    }
    async function react(m,emoji) {
      const id=active;
      try{
        const d=await write('reactions',{message_id:m.id,emoji});
        if(!alive)return;if(d.message)replaceMessage(d.message);else if(Array.isArray(d.reactions))replaceMessage({...m,reactions:d.reactions});else await refreshActive();
      }catch(error){if(error.status===403)invalidateRoom(id,error);else notify(error.message);}
    }
    function auxHeader(title,closeLabel='Back to conversation') {
      const h=el('header','cv4-aux-header');h.append(el('h3','',title),button(closeLabel,'close',closeAux,'cv4-icon'));return h;
    }
    function openAux(kind) {
      stopRecording(true);saveCurrentDrafts();for(const c of composers){closeToolPicker(c);if(c.rootId){releaseVoicePreview(c);composers.delete(c);}}
      ++searchSerial;aux={kind};auxiliary.hidden=false;auxiliary.replaceChildren();root.dataset.aux=kind;setPane('aux');sendTyping(false);
      // A thread is the only auxiliary pane that can mark message rows read.
      queueRead();
    }
    function closeAux() {
      if(!aux)return;saveCurrentDrafts();stopRecording(true);if(aux.kind==='calls')window.cvConnectCalls?.unmount?.();++searchSerial;aux=null;auxiliary.hidden=true;auxiliary.querySelectorAll('video,audio').forEach(v=>v.pause());auxiliary.replaceChildren();delete root.dataset.aux;
      for(const c of composers)if(c.rootId){closeToolPicker(c);releaseVoicePreview(c);composers.delete(c);}
      setPane(section==='people'?'people':active?'conversation':'sidebar');queueRead();
    }
    async function openThread(rootId) {
      const id=active;if(!id)return;openAux('thread');aux.rootId=rootId;auxiliary.append(auxHeader('Thread'));
      const log=el('div','cv4-thread-log');log.setAttribute('role','log');log.setAttribute('aria-label','Thread replies');log.tabIndex=0;auxiliary.append(log);skeleton(log,'Loading replies…');
      const compose=createComposer(id,rootId);auxiliary.append(compose.form);
      try{const st=await loadMessages(id,{root:rootId});if(!alive||active!==id||aux?.rootId!==rootId)return;paintThread(st,log);compose.input.focus();}
      catch(error){if(error.status===403)invalidateRoom(id,error);else if(aux?.rootId===rootId){log.removeAttribute('aria-busy');log.replaceChildren(empty('Replies could not load',error.message,button('Retry','refresh',()=>openThread(rootId))));}}
    }
    function paintThread(st,log) {
      const rootMessage=stateFor(active).messages.find(m=>m.id===aux?.rootId), view={...st,messages:st.messages.filter(m=>m.id!==rootMessage?.id)};
      if(rootMessage)view.messages=[rootMessage,...view.messages];paintMessages(log,view,true);
    }
    function showEdit(m) {
      openAux('edit');auxiliary.append(auxHeader('Edit your message'));
      const form=el('form','cv4-detail-form'),label=el('label','','Message'),input=el('textarea','cv4-input'),error=el('p','cv4-form-status');
      input.maxLength=4000;input.value=m.body;input.rows=5;input.id=instanceId+'-edit';label.htmlFor=input.id;error.setAttribute('role','status');
      const save=button('Save changes','check',null,'cv4-primary');save.type='submit';
      const cancelButton=button('Cancel',null,closeAux);form.append(label,input,error,save,cancelButton);auxiliary.append(form);input.focus();
      form.addEventListener('submit',async e=>{
        e.preventDefault();if(!input.value.trim()){error.textContent='Write a message before saving.';return;}
        save.disabled=true;const id=active;
        try{const d=await write('messages/edit',{id:m.id,body:input.value,version:m.version});if(alive&&active===id){if(aux?.kind==='edit'&&form.isConnected)closeAux();if(d.message)replaceMessage(d.message);else await refreshActive();announce('Message updated.');}}
        catch(err){if(err.status===403)invalidateRoom(id,err);else{error.textContent=err.message;if(err.status===409)await refreshActive().catch(()=>{});}}finally{save.disabled=false;}
      });
    }
    function showDelete(m) {
      openAux('delete');auxiliary.append(auxHeader('Delete your message'));
      const body=el('div','cv4-detail-form'),error=el('p','cv4-form-status');error.setAttribute('role','status');
      body.append(el('p','','Delete this message? A “message deleted” marker will remain in the conversation.'),el('blockquote','',m.body));
      const confirm=button('Delete message','trash',async()=>{
        confirm.disabled=true;const id=active;
        try{const d=await write('messages/delete',{id:m.id,version:m.version});if(alive&&active===id){if(aux?.kind==='delete'&&body.isConnected)closeAux();if(d.message)replaceMessage(d.message);else await refreshActive();announce('Message deleted.');}}
        catch(err){if(err.status===403)invalidateRoom(id,err);else{error.textContent=err.message;if(err.status===409)await refreshActive().catch(()=>{});}}finally{confirm.disabled=false;}
      },'cv4-danger');confirm.dataset.action='confirm-delete';body.append(error,confirm,button('Cancel',null,closeAux));auxiliary.append(body);
    }
    function draftKey(id,rootId='') { return text(person?.id)+':'+id+':'+rootId; }
    function toolDocument(raw) {
      const id=text(raw?.document_id||raw?.id),url=recordURL(raw?.url);
      if(!/^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._:-]{0,119}$/.test(id)||!url)return null;
      const target=new URL(url,location.origin);
      if(target.pathname!=='/communiverse/workspace/'||target.searchParams.get('view')!=='manage'||target.searchParams.get('section')!=='tools'||target.searchParams.get('document')!==id||['view','section','document'].some(key=>target.searchParams.getAll(key).length!==1))return null;
      if(target.hash||[...target.searchParams.keys()].some(key=>!['view','section','document','row','column'].includes(key)))return null;
      return {id,title:text(raw.title||'Tools document').slice(0,160),kind:text(raw.document_kind||raw.kind||'document').slice(0,40),version:raw.version??raw.document_version??null,scope:text(raw.scope).slice(0,80),url};
    }
    function toolKind(kind) { return ({sheet:'Sheet',note:'Note',page:'Page',notebook:'Notebook',folder:'Folder',row:'Sheet row'})[kind]||'Document'; }
    function toolSourceCard(raw,version) {
      const card=el('article','cv4-answer-record cv4-tool-source'),ref=toolDocument(raw);
      if(!ref){card.dataset.sourceState='unavailable';card.append(el('h5','','Source unavailable'),el('p','','This source cannot be opened with the available access.'));return card;}
      card.dataset.document=ref.id;
      const revision=version??ref.version;
      card.append(el('small','',toolKind(ref.kind)+(revision!=null?' · Version '+text(revision).slice(0,24):'')),el('h5','',ref.title));
      // Descriptions come only from the permission-filtered server record.
      if(typeof raw.description==='string'&&raw.description)card.append(el('p','',raw.description.slice(0,1200)));
      card.append(el('small','cv4-tool-scope','Only shared with this conversation'));
      const actions=el('div','cv4-source-actions'),open=el('a','cv4-button','Open '+toolKind(ref.kind).toLowerCase());open.href=ref.url;open.dataset.action='open-tool';
      const ask=button('Ask privately',null,()=>openPrivateTool(ref));ask.dataset.action='private-tool';ask.setAttribute('aria-label','Ask privately about '+ref.title);
      actions.append(open,ask);card.append(actions);return card;
    }
    function openPrivateTool(ref,prompt='') {
      if(!alive||sessionLocked||!person)return;
      const document=toolDocument(ref);if(!document){notify('This source is unavailable.');return;}
      if(typeof window.cvCosmos?.openContext!=='function'){notify('Private document context is unavailable here. Open this source in Tools to ask Cosmos.');return;}
      window.cvCosmos.openContext({page:document.url,title:document.title,prompt:text(prompt||'Help me understand this document and suggest useful next steps.').slice(0,2000)});
    }
    function composerReadOnly(ctx) {
      const c=conversation(ctx.id);
      return c?.read_only===true||c?.readOnly===true||c?.can_send===false||c?.canSend===false||capabilities.sendMessages===false;
    }
    function selectedDocumentIds(ctx) { return list(ctx.documents).slice(0,3).map(d=>d.id); }
    function closeToolPicker(ctx,focus=false) {
      const ui=ctx.toolsUI;if(!ui||ui.panel.hidden&&!ui.loading&&!ui.rows.length)return;
      ++ui.generation;ui.abort?.abort();ui.abort=null;ui.loading=false;ui.rows=[];ui.cursor=null;ui.choices.clear();
      ui.panel.hidden=true;ui.results.replaceChildren();ui.results.removeAttribute('aria-busy');ctx.tools.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
      if(focus&&voiceContextActive(ctx))ctx.tools.focus();
    }
    function paintToolSelection(ctx) {
      if(!ctx.toolSelection)return;
      const readonly=composerReadOnly(ctx),busy=ctx.pending||!!ctx.voice;
      ctx.tools.disabled=busy||readonly;ctx.toolSelection.hidden=!!ctx.voice||!ctx.documents.length;
      if(ctx.send)ctx.send.disabled=busy||readonly||!!(ctx.documents.length&&ctx.toolsBlocked);
      ctx.toolSelection.replaceChildren();
      if(ctx.documents.length){
        ctx.toolSelection.append(el('small','cv4-tool-scope','Only shared with this conversation · access checked when you send'));
        const chips=el('div','cv4-tool-chips');chips.tabIndex=0;chips.setAttribute('aria-label','Selected Tools references');
        for(const ref of ctx.documents){
          const chip=el('div','cv4-tool-chip');chip.dataset.document=ref.id;
          const copy=el('div','cv4-tool-chip-copy');copy.append(el('span','',ref.title),el('small','',toolKind(ref.kind)+(ref.version!=null?' · Version '+text(ref.version).slice(0,24):'')));
          const remove=button('Remove','close',()=>{
            if(ctx.pending||ctx.voice||composerReadOnly(ctx)||!voiceContextActive(ctx))return;
            ctx.documents=ctx.documents.filter(d=>d.id!==ref.id);ctx.requestId='';ctx.fingerprint='';storeDraft(ctx);paintToolSelection(ctx);ctx.tools.focus();announce('Tools reference removed.');
          });remove.dataset.action='remove-tool';remove.setAttribute('aria-label','Remove '+ref.title);remove.disabled=busy||readonly;
          const ask=button('Ask privately',null,()=>openPrivateTool(ref,ctx.isCosmos?ctx.input.value:''));ask.dataset.action='private-tool';ask.setAttribute('aria-label','Ask privately about '+ref.title);ask.disabled=ctx.pending;
          chip.append(icon('document'),copy,remove,ask);chips.append(chip);
        }ctx.toolSelection.append(chips);
      }
      const ui=ctx.toolsUI;if(!ui)return;
      ui.count.textContent=ctx.documents.length+' of 3 selected';
      for(const [id,choice]of ui.choices){choice.checked=ctx.documents.some(d=>d.id===id);choice.disabled=busy||readonly||!!ctx.toolsBlocked||(!choice.checked&&ctx.documents.length>=3);}
      ui.more.disabled=ui.loading||ctx.pending;ui.retry.disabled=ui.loading||ctx.pending;
    }
    function createToolSelection(ctx) {
      ctx.documents=list(ctx.documents).slice(0,3);
      const tools=button('Tools','document',()=>{
        if(ctx.pending||ctx.voice||composerReadOnly(ctx)||!voiceContextActive(ctx))return;
        if(!ui.panel.hidden){closeToolPicker(ctx,true);return;}
        ui.panel.hidden=false;tools.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');heading.focus();loadToolChoices(ctx);
      });tools.dataset.action='tools';tools.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
