async function renderConnect(seq){const sectionName=connectSection,contents=$('#cv-ws-content');contents.innerHTML=subnav([['messages','Messages'],['meetings','Meetings']],sectionName,'connect')+empty('Loading…');try{if(sectionName==='messages'){const d=await connect('threads');if(seq!==renderSequence)return;connectRows=d.threads||[];contents.innerHTML=subnav([['messages','Messages'],['meetings','Meetings']],sectionName,'connect')+section('A conversation moves things forward.',`<div class="cv-ws-list">${connectRows.filter(matches).map(t=>`<article class="cv-ws-row"><button data-ws-thread="${esc(t.id)}"><strong>${esc(t.title)}</strong><p>${esc((t.participants||[]).map(p=>p.name).join(', '))}</p><p>${esc(t.last_message?.body||t.last_message||'Start with a clear next step.')}</p></button>${t.unread?`<span class="cv-ws-pill">${Number(t.unread)} new</span>`:''}<span>${esc(clockDate(t.updated_at))}</span></article>`).join('')||empty('Start a conversation with a teammate or an artist. Replies and shared files stay together.')}</div>`,'<button class="cv-button" data-ws-thread-new>New conversation</button>')}else{const d=await connect('meetings');if(seq!==renderSequence)return;meetingRows=d.meetings||[];contents.innerHTML=subnav([['messages','Messages'],['meetings','Meetings']],sectionName,'connect')+section('Make time for the work.',`<div class="cv-ws-list">${meetingRows.filter(matches).map(m=>`<article class="cv-ws-row"><button data-ws-meeting="${esc(m.id)}"><strong>${esc(m.title)}</strong><p>${esc(clockDate(m.start_at,m.timezone))} · ${esc(m.timezone)}</p><p>${esc((m.participants||[]).map(p=>p.name).join(', '))}</p></button>${pill(m.status)}<span>${esc(m.location||'Location to follow')}</span></article>`).join('')||empty('Meetings, responses and invitation delivery will appear here.')}</div>`,'<button class="cv-button" data-ws-meeting-new>Schedule a meeting</button>')}}catch(e){if(seq===renderSequence)contents.innerHTML=empty(e.message)}}
function threadForm(selected=[]){if(window.cvChat){$('#cv-ws-dialog').close();return window.cvChat.compose(selected);}currentType='thread-new';modal('Bring the right people together.',`<form data-ws-enhanced="thread"><label>Conversation title<input name="title" maxlength="120" required></label>${participantChecks(selected)}<label>First message<textarea name="body" rows="4" maxlength="4000" required></textarea></label><button class="cv-button">Start conversation</button><p role="status"></p></form>`)}
const attachmentList=(rows=[],prefix='workspace/connect/files/file')=>`<div class="cv-ws-attachments">${rows.map(f=>`<a href="/communiverse/api/${prefix}?id=${esc(f.id)}" download><span>${esc(f.name)}${(f.tags||[]).length?`<small>${esc((Array.isArray(f.tags)?f.tags:tags(f.tags)).join(' · '))}</small>`:''}</span><span>${Math.round(f.bytes/1024)} KB</span></a>`).join('')||'<p class="cv-ws-meta">No files here yet.</p>'}</div>`;
function fileForm(target,type='connect'){return `<form data-ws-enhanced="file" data-target="${esc(target)}" data-file-type="${type}"><div class="cv-form-row"><label>File<input name="file" type="file" required></label><label>Tags <span>(separate with commas)</span><input name="tags" maxlength="300" placeholder="Craft, process, reference"></label></div><p class="cv-ws-meta">Documents and images up to 20 MB. Craft videos up to 32 MB.</p><button class="cv-quiet-button">Attach file</button><p role="status"></p></form>`}
async function openThread(id,reply=''){if(window.cvWorkspaceV4){$('#cv-ws-dialog').close();window.cvWorkspaceV4.openConversation(id);return;}if(window.cvChat){$('#cv-ws-dialog').close();return window.cvChat.open(id,{reply});}const savedDraft=currentType==='thread'&&currentTarget===id?$('form[data-ws-enhanced=message] textarea')?.value||'':'';const d=await connect('threads?id='+encodeURIComponent(id));threadDetail=d;await connect('threads/read',{id});currentTarget=id;currentType='thread';modal(d.thread.title,`<p class="cv-ws-meta">${esc((d.participants||d.thread.participants||[]).map(p=>p.name).join(', '))}</p><div class="cv-ws-conversation">${(d.messages||[]).map(m=>`<article data-message-id="${esc(m.id)}" class="cv-ws-message ${m.sender_id===state.person.id?'is-self':''}"><div class="cv-ws-message-person">${photo(getPerson(m.sender_id))}<strong>${esc(getPerson(m.sender_id).name)}</strong><span>${esc(clockDate(m.created_at))}</span></div>${m.reply_to?`<blockquote>${esc(d.messages.find(x=>x.id===m.reply_to)?.body?.slice(0,140)||'Earlier message')}</blockquote>`:''}<p>${esc(m.body)}</p><button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-reply="${esc(m.id)}">Reply</button></article>`).join('')}</div><form data-ws-enhanced="message"><input type="hidden" name="thread_id" value="${esc(id)}"><input type="hidden" name="reply_to" value="${esc(reply)}">${reply?`<p class="cv-ws-meta">Replying to ${esc(getPerson(d.messages.find(x=>x.id===reply)?.sender_id).name)} <button type="button" class="cv-text-link" data-ws-reply-clear>Cancel reply</button></p>`:''}<label>Your message<textarea name="body" rows="3" maxlength="4000" required></textarea></label><button class="cv-button">Send message</button><p role="status"></p></form><h3>Shared files</h3>${attachmentList(d.files)}${fileForm(id)}`);const composer=$('form[data-ws-enhanced=message] textarea');if(composer)composer.value=savedDraft;if(reply)composer?.focus()}
function meetingForm(selected=[]){currentType='meeting-new';modal('A little time together.',`<form data-ws-enhanced="meeting"><label>Meeting title<input name="title" maxlength="120" required></label>${participantChecks(selected)}<div class="cv-form-row"><label>Starts <span>(your local time)</span><input name="start_at" type="datetime-local" required></label><label>Ends <span>(your local time)</span><input name="end_at" type="datetime-local" required></label></div><label>Time zone<input name="timezone" value="${esc(Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone)}" maxlength="80" required></label><label>Where?<input name="location" maxlength="240" placeholder="Studio, office or a meeting link"></label><label>Agenda<textarea name="note" rows="3" maxlength="2000"></textarea></label>${aiButton('meeting-agenda','note')}<p class="cv-ws-meta">Invitations go to confirmed email addresses. Anyone who hasn’t added one will still see the meeting here.</p><button class="cv-button">Schedule and send invitations</button><p role="status"></p></form>`)}
async function openMeeting(id){const d=await connect('meetings');meetingRows=d.meetings||[];const m=meetingRows.find(m=>m.id===id);if(!m)throw Error('This meeting is unavailable.');currentTarget=id;currentType='meeting';const self=(m.participants||[]).find(p=>p.id===state.person.id),files=await connect('files?target='+encodeURIComponent(id));modal(m.title,`<div class="cv-ws-calendar-date"><strong>${esc(clockDate(m.start_at,m.timezone))}</strong><span>${esc(clockDate(m.end_at,m.timezone))} · ${esc(m.timezone)}</span></div>${pill(m.status)}<a class="cv-quiet-button cv-ws-public-profile" href="/communiverse/api/workspace/connect/meetings/calendar?id=${esc(m.id)}" download>Add to calendar</a><p>${esc(m.location)}</p><p class="cv-ws-note">${esc(m.note)}</p><div class="cv-ws-list">${(m.participants||[]).map(p=>`<article class="cv-ws-person">${photo(getPerson(p.id))}<div><h3>${esc(p.name)}</h3>${pill(p.response||'invited')}<p>${esc(({'accepted-by-provider':'Invitation accepted for sending',sent:'Invitation emailed',pending:'Email queued','waiting-contact':'Add a confirmed meeting email',no_email:'Add a confirmed meeting email',failed:'Email needs another attempt'}[(m.delivery||[]).find(x=>x.identity_id===p.id)?.state])||'Invitation delivery recorded')}</p></div></article>`).join('')}</div>${m.status!=='cancelled'&&self?`<form data-ws-enhanced="meeting-response"><input name="id" type="hidden" value="${esc(m.id)}"><div class="cv-ws-detail-actions"><button class="cv-button" name="response" value="accepted">Accept</button><button class="cv-quiet-button" name="response" value="declined">Decline</button></div><p role="status"></p></form>`:''}${m.status!=='cancelled'&&m.created_by===state.person.id?`<button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-meeting-cancel="${esc(m.id)}">Cancel meeting</button>`:''}<h3>Meeting files</h3>${attachmentList(files.files)}${fileForm(id)}`)}
async function artistOverview(seq){try{if(!studioId)studioId=studioIndex.artists[0]?.id||studioIndex.artists[0]?.artist_id||'';if(!studioId){$('#cv-ws-content').innerHTML=empty('Your artist workspace is being connected to your public circle.');return}const d=await studio('dashboard?artist_id='+encodeURIComponent(studioId));if(seq!==renderSequence)return;studioData=d;$('#cv-ws-content').innerHTML=`<div class="cv-ws-momentum">${photo(state.person)}<div><h2>Room for your craft to grow.</h2><p>Your story, your schedule and your work share one studio.</p></div><button class="cv-button" data-ws-studio-open>Open my studio</button></div>`+studioSummary(d)+section('A few useful next steps',`<div class="cv-ws-grid"><article class="cv-ws-card"><h3>Make yourself available</h3><p>Set a place, a date and a workshop price.</p><button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-studio-go="availability">Manage availability</button></article><article class="cv-ws-card"><h3>Bring your work together</h3><p>Record products and upload your making process.</p><button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-studio-go="products">Manage products</button></article><article class="cv-ws-card"><h3>See what’s growing</h3><p>Keep your sales in one place and discover where your clients are.</p><button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-studio-go="sales">View sales</button></article></div>`)}catch(e){if(seq===renderSequence)$('#cv-ws-content').innerHTML=empty(e.message)}}
function studioSummary(d){const revenue=Array.isArray(d.revenue)?d.revenue:Object.entries(d.revenue||{}).map(([currency,value])=>({currency,...(typeof value==='object'?value:{paid:value})}));return `<div class="cv-ws-stats">${stat((d.products||[]).length,'Products recorded')}${stat((d.products||[]).reduce((n,p)=>n+Number(p.stock||0),0),'Pieces in stock')}${stat((d.slots||[]).filter(s=>s.status==='active'&&new Date(s.start_at)>new Date()).length,'Upcoming dates')}${stat((d.transactions||[]).filter(t=>t.status==='paid').length,'Paid sales & sessions')}</div><div class="cv-ws-section-heading"><h2>Income recorded</h2><span class="cv-ws-meta">Paid records · refunds deducted</span></div>${revenue.length?`<div class="cv-ws-revenue">${revenue.map(r=>`<article class="cv-ws-card"><p class="cv-kicker">${esc(r.currency||'USD')}</p><strong>${esc(money(r.total??r.net??r.net_revenue??r.paid??r.gross??0,r.currency))}</strong><p>${esc(r.currency)} totals stay separate.</p></article>`).join('')}</div>`:empty('Your income will appear as paid product sales or workshops are recorded.')}`}
function seriesChart(d){const rows=Array.isArray(d.series)?d.series:[],currencies=[...new Set(rows.map(r=>r.currency||'USD'))];if(!rows.length||!rows.some(r=>Number(r.net??r.gross??r.revenue??r.total??0)))return empty('Your monthly income chart grows with your recorded sales.');return currencies.map(c=>{const points=rows.filter(r=>(r.currency||'USD')===c),max=Math.max(...points.map(p=>Number(p.net??p.gross??p.revenue??p.total??0)),1);return `<article class="cv-ws-card cv-ws-chart"><h3>Income by month · ${esc(c)}</h3><div class="cv-ws-bars">${points.map(p=>{const n=Number(p.net??p.gross??p.revenue??p.total??0),date=p.month||p.label;return `<div class="cv-ws-bar-item"><span>${esc(money(n,c))}</span><div class="cv-ws-bar-track"><i style="height:${Math.max(2,100*n/max)}%"></i></div><small>${esc(date)}</small></div>`}).join('')}</div><details><summary>View numbers</summary><dl>${points.map(p=>`<div><dt>${esc(p.month||p.label)}</dt><dd>${esc(money(p.net??p.gross??p.revenue??p.total??0,c))}</dd></div>`).join('')}</dl></details></article>`}).join('')}
async function renderStudio(seq){const content=$('#cv-ws-content'),staff=state.person.role!=='artist';let navitems=staff?[['business','Artist business'],['pipeline','Pipeline'],...(state.permissions.admin?[['sessions','Group requests']]:[])]:[['business','Overview'],['availability','Availability'],['products','Products'],['sales','Sales'],['files','Files']];if(staff&&studioIndex.artists.length)navitems=[['business','Artist business'],['availability','Availability'],['products','Products'],['sales','Sales'],['files','Files'],['pipeline','Pipeline'],...(state.permissions.admin?[['sessions','Group requests']]:[])];if(!studioIndex.permissions.finance)navitems=navitems.filter(([v])=>v!=='sales');content.innerHTML=subnav(navitems,studioSection,'studio');if(studioSection==='pipeline'){await renderPipeline();if(seq===renderSequence)content.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',subnav(navitems,studioSection,'studio'));return}if(studioSection==='sessions'){await loadSessions();if(seq===renderSequence)content.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin',subnav(navitems,studioSection,'studio'));return}const artists=studioIndex.artists||[];if(!artists.length){content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',empty(staff?'Artist business records appear when artists are connected to your programme. The pipeline remains available.':'Your studio is being connected to your artist profile.'));return}if(!artists.some(a=>(a.id||a.artist_id)===studioId))studioId=artists[0].id||artists[0].artist_id;try{const d=await studio('dashboard?artist_id='+encodeURIComponent(studioId));if(seq!==renderSequence)return;studioData=d;const p=d.profile||{},artist=artists.find(a=>(a.id||a.artist_id)===studioId)||{};content.innerHTML=subnav(navitems,studioSection,'studio')+`<div class="cv-ws-studio-heading"><div class="cv-ws-artist-heading">${photo({name:artist.title||artist.name,image:artist.image,photo:artist.photo||artist.image})}<div><h2>${esc(artist.title||artist.name||p.name||'Your studio')}</h2><p>${esc(p.craft||artist.craft||'')} ${p.city?'· '+esc(p.city):''}</p></div></div>${artists.length>1?`<label class="cv-ws-artist-switch">Artist<select id="cv-ws-studio-select">${options(artists.map(a=>[a.id||a.artist_id,a.title||a.name||a.id]),studioId)}</select></label>`:''}${studioIndex.permissions.editBusiness?'<button class="cv-quiet-button" data-ws-studio-profile>Studio details</button>':''}${studioIndex.permissions.assign?'<button class="cv-quiet-button" data-ws-studio-assignment>Connections</button>':''}</div>`;
if(studioSection==='business'){content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',studioIndex.permissions.finance?studioSummary(d)+section('The shape of your income',seriesChart(d))+section('Where your clients are',demographicChart(d)):section('Your craft story',`<p class="cv-ws-note">${esc(p.bio||'This artist’s story is being brought together.')}</p>`));content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',section('Small moves, useful growth',`<div class="cv-ws-grid">${(d.insights||[]).map(i=>`<article class="cv-ws-card"><h3>${esc(i.title||'A next step')}</h3><p>${esc(i.body||i.description||i)}</p></article>`).join('')||'<article class="cv-ws-card"><h3>Keep the picture complete</h3><p>Record products, paid sales and confirmed dates to see useful patterns here.</p></article>'}</div>`));if(studioIndex.permissions.earnings)showEarnings().catch(e=>error(e));}
else if(studioSection==='availability')content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',section('Make room for a workshop.',`<p class="cv-ws-meta">${p.can_host?'Your studio can host workshops.':'Set your workshop hosting details before publishing a date.'}</p><div class="cv-ws-list">${(d.slots||[]).filter(s=>s.status==='active').map(s=>`<article class="cv-ws-row"><span><strong>${esc(clockDate(s.start_at,s.timezone))}</strong><p>${esc(s.city)} · ${esc(s.timezone)} · ${Number(s.capacity)} people</p></span><span>${esc(money(s.price??s.price_usd,s.currency||'USD'))} / person</span>${studioIndex.permissions.publishSlots&&s.version?`<button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-studio-slot-hide="${esc(s.id)}">Hide date</button>`:''}</article>`).join('')||empty('Publish a date when the location, capacity and price are ready.')}</div>`,studioIndex.permissions.publishSlots?'<button class="cv-button" data-ws-studio-slot-new>Add availability</button>':''));
else if(studioSection==='products')content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',section('The work you make.',`<div class="cv-ws-grid">${(d.products||[]).filter(matches).map(p=>`<article class="cv-ws-card"><p class="cv-kicker">${esc(p.sku||'Piece')}</p><h3>${esc(p.title)}</h3><p>${esc(money(p.price,p.currency))} · ${Number(p.stock)} in stock</p>${pill(p.status)}<p>${esc((p.tags||[]).join?.(' · ')||p.tags||'')}</p>${studioIndex.permissions.editBusiness?`<button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-studio-product="${esc(p.id)}">Edit product</button>`:''}</article>`).join('')||empty('Add a product, its price and the pieces you have available.')}</div>`,studioIndex.permissions.editBusiness?'<button class="cv-button" data-ws-studio-product-new>Add a product</button>':''));
else if(studioSection==='sales')content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',section('A record of what’s growing.',`<p class="cv-ws-meta">Paid sales count towards your income. Pending records remain separate.</p><div class="cv-ws-list">${(d.transactions||[]).map(t=>`<article class="cv-ws-row"><button data-ws-studio-sale="${esc(t.id)}">${esc(label(t.kind))}<p>${esc(when(t.occurred_on))} · ${Number(t.quantity)} ${Number(t.quantity)===1?'piece or place':'pieces or places'}</p></button>${pill(t.status)}<span>${esc(money(Number(t.gross)-Number(t.refund||0),t.currency))}</span></article>`).join('')||empty('Record a sale or a workshop payment to keep your income organised.')}</div>`,'<button class="cv-button" data-ws-studio-sale-new>Record a sale</button>'));
else if(studioSection==='files'){const [f,w]=await Promise.all([connect('files?target='+encodeURIComponent('artist:'+studioId)),studio('published?artist_id='+encodeURIComponent(studioId))]);if(seq!==renderSequence)return;studioFiles=f.files||[];publishedWorks=w.works||[];content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',section('The work behind the work.',studioMediaLibrary(studioFiles,publishedWorks)+fileForm(studioId,'studio')))}
}catch(e){if(seq===renderSequence)content.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',empty(e.message))}}
function demographicChart(d){const rows=Array.isArray(d.demographics)?d.demographics:d.demographics?.locations||d.demographics?.countries||[];if(!rows.length)return empty('Client locations appear only when a client has agreed to share them.');const total=rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+Number(r.sales||r.count||r.clients||0),0);return `<article class="cv-ws-card"><p class="cv-ws-meta">Shared client locations · ${total} recorded</p><div class="cv-ws-geography">${rows.map(r=>{const n=Number(r.sales||r.count||r.clients||0);return `<div><strong>${esc([r.city,r.country||r.location].filter(Boolean).join(', '))}</strong><span>${n}</span><progress max="${total||1}" value="${n}" aria-label="${esc(r.country||r.location)}: ${n} clients"></progress></div>`}).join('')}</div></article>`}

attached</option>${options((studioData.products||[]).map(p=>[p.id,p.title]),t?.product_id||'')}</select></label><div class="cv-form-row"><label>Pieces / places<input type="number" name="quantity" min="1" max="1000000" value="${Number(t?.quantity||1)}" required></label><label>Date<input type="date" name="occurred_on" value="${esc(t?.occurred_on||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10))}" required></label></div><div class="cv-form-row"><label>Gross amount<input type="number" name="gross" min="0" max="100000000" step=".01" value="${t?.gross??''}" required></label><label>Refund<input let connectPollBusy=false;async function pollConnections(){if(connectPollBusy||document.hidden||!state||$('#cv-ws-app').hidden)return;connectPollBusy=true;try{const unread=await connect('notifications'),fresh=unread.notifications||[];state.notifications=[...state.notifications.filter(n=>!connectUnreadIds.has(n.id)),...fresh];connectUnreadIds=new Set(fresh.map(n=>n.id));$('#cv-ws-unread').textContent=state.notifications.length?'('+state.notifications.length+')':'';if(currentType==='thread'&&$('#cv-ws-dialog').open&&threadDetail&&currentTarget){const threadId=currentTarget,d=await connect('threads?id='+encodeURIComponent(threadId)),box=$('.cv-ws-conversation');if(currentType!=='thread'||!$('#cv-ws-dialog').open||currentTarget!==threadId||!box)return;const known=new Set([...box.querySelectorAll('[data-message-id]')].map(n=>n.dataset.messageId));for(const m of d.messages||[])if(!known.has(m.id))box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`<article data-message-id="${esc(m.id)}" class="cv-ws-message ${m.sender_id===state.person.id?'is-self':''}"><div class="cv-ws-message-person">${photo(getPerson(m.sender_id))}<strong>${esc(getPerson(m.sender_id).name)}</strong><span>${esc(clockDate(m.created_at))}</span></div>${m.reply_to?`<blockquote>${esc(d.messages.find(x=>x.id===m.reply_to)?.body?.slice(0,200)||'An earlier message')}</blockquote>`:''}<p>${esc(m.body)}</p><button class="cv-text-link" data-ws-reply="${esc(m.id)}">Reply</button></article>`);threadDetail=d;await connect('threads/read',{id:threadId});}}catch{}finally{connectPollBusy=false}}setInterval(pollConnections,20000);document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(!document.hidden)pollConnections()});

(function nativeAccountHooks(adapter,{root,app,dialog,profileForm}) {
  adapter.profile=async()=>{
    if(!adapter.getState()?.person||app.hidden||dialog.open)return false;
    await profileForm();
    return dialog.open;
  };
  adapter.signOut=()=>{
    if(!adapter.getState()?.person||app.hidden)return false;
    const button=root.querySelector('[data-ws-logout]');
    if(!button)return false;
    button.click();
    return true;
  };
})(window.__cvWorkspaceNative,{root,app:$('#cv-ws-app'),dialog:$('#cv-ws-dialog'),profileForm});})();
